### [1] [The Art of Orbifolding by Huge Non-Abelian Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36024)
*Jan Albert,Masaki Okada,Yuji Tachikawa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了由包含 Conway 群（散在有限单群之一）的巨大非阿贝尔群所作用的二维手征费米子轨形的环面配分函数，并系统解决了这一看似标准的问题中此前未解决的原理性与实践性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于寻找拓扑模形式（topological modular forms）的周期性元素。为解释为何需要发展本文中的全部技术，作者对这一问题作了简要介绍；所选取的具体轨形正是出于这一搜索目标。

Method: 作者发展了计算巨大非阿贝尔群轨形的环面配分函数的一般性方法，重点处理了包含 Conway 群的二维手征费米子轨形，克服了原理层面和实践层面的多重难点。这些方法具有普适性。

Result: 作者成功计算了所述轨形的环面配分函数，并详细描述了如何克服此前未解决的多重重大问题；但遗憾的是，所考虑的轨形最终被证明并非拓扑模形式的周期性元素。

Conclusion: 尽管本文发展的方法具有一般适用性，但由寻找周期性元素这一目标所选定的轨形结果并不符合预期，未能找到所期望的拓扑模形式周期性元素。

Abstract: We compute torus partition functions of orbifolds of two-dimensional chiral fermions by huge non-Abelian groups involving the Conway group, one of the sporadic finite simple groups. This seemingly standard problem turns out to contain, somewhat unexpectedly, multiple major issues hitherto unresolved, both at the level of the principle and at the level of practice. We describe in detail how they can be overcome.
  Although the methods described in this paper are generally applicable, the choice of the particular orbifolds we consider in this paper was motivated by the search for the periodicity element of topological modular forms. We will provide a brief introduction to this question, to explain why all the techniques in this paper had to be developed. Unfortunately, the orbifolds we considered turned out not to be the periodicity element.

</details>


### [2] [Instanton Structure from 325-Loop 3D $φ^3$ Perturbation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36025)
*Ian Moult,Bo Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于热带场论的退火重要性采样框架，用于克服高阶微扰计算中的指数权重方差障碍，并在三维大质量φ³理论中计算到325圈的微扰级数，从而以0.03%精度确定instanton作用量并验证相关非微扰物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微扰论的高阶行为能为量子场论中的非微扰现象（如instanton）提供重要信息，但传统Feynman图展开在现实场论中复杂度极高，难以推进到高阶。作者希望借助热带曲线模空间全局采样的最新进展，构建可精确计算到高阶的数值方法。

Method: 基于热带曲线模空间的全局采样，引入一种面向热带场论的退火重要性采样（annealed importance sampling）框架，克服指数权重方差障碍，从而实现对微扰级数高阶系数的精确数值计算。

Result: 将该方法应用于三维大质量φ³理论，计算了二点函数直到325圈（loops）的微扰级数；由此确定instanton作用量，精度达0.03%，并确认集体坐标指数，提取了单圈行列式及至四圈的instanton涨落修正，结果与直接半经典计算高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该退火重要性采样方法可推广至多种相关场论，为定量研究其高圈行为提供了可行的数值工具，弥补了传统Feynman图展开在高阶区域难以计算的不足。

Abstract: The high order behavior of perturbation theory provides insight into non-perturbative phenomenon in quantum field theory. While extensively studied in simplified models, high order behavior is challenging to access in more realistic field theories due to the complexity of the standard Feynman diagrammatic expansion. Building on recent progress in global sampling on the moduli space of tropical curves, we introduce an annealed importance sampling framework for tropical field theory. Our approach overcomes the exponential weight-variance barrier, allowing precise numerical calculations to high orders. We apply our technique to compute the perturbative series for the two-point function in the massive three-dimensional $φ^3$ theory through 325 loops. These coefficients determine the instanton action in $φ^3$ theory to 0.03\% precision, confirm the collective-coordinate exponent, and extract the one-loop determinant and the instanton fluctuation corrections through four loops, in remarkable agreement with direct semiclassical calculations. Our approach is applicable to a variety of related field theories, facilitating the quantitative exploration of their high loop behavior.

</details>


### [3] [Cusp regularization with spin structures and gravitational anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36028)
*Elisa Tabor,Cynthia Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 SL(2,Z) 黑洞族的 cusp 正则化及其对自旋结构的依赖，发现引力反常导致黑洞贡献相互抵消，从而移除主要 cusp 发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SL(2,Z) 黑洞族在尖点（cusp）几何处存在累积点，导致对所有黑洞求和的发散；需要将 cusp 正则化推广到固定边界自旋结构的情形，并理解引力反常如何影响发散结构。

Method: 将 cusp 正则化扩展到与固定边界自旋结构相容的几何求和中；对 c_L=c_R 情形引入 cusp 作为抵消项；计算引力反常引入的、依赖自旋结构的反常相位，分析黑洞贡献间的相互抵消机制。

Result: 对 c_L=c_R，在三种偶边界自旋结构下，cusp 抵消项都能消去主要发散，且三个扇形所需的减除项相同，为玻色情形的三分之二。引力反常会导致黑洞贡献之间的相消，从而在纯引力中除 c_L-c_R=0,±24，在 N=(1,1) 超引力中除 c_L-c_R=0,±12 外移除了主要 cusp 发散。

Conclusion: cusp 正则化可成功推广到边界自旋结构固定的情形；引力反常引发的相消显著改变发散结构，仅在少数特定中心荷差处保留主要发散，并给出了负责这些相消的、依赖自旋结构的反常相位的具体形式。

Abstract: The family of $SL(2,\mathbb Z)$ black holes has an accumulation point corresponding to a cusped geometry, which causes the sum over black holes to diverge. We extend cusp regularization to the sum over geometries compatible with a fixed boundary spin structure. For $c_L=c_R$, including the cusp as a counterterm cancels the leading divergence for each of the three even boundary spin structures. The required subtraction is the same in all three sectors and is two thirds of the bosonic one. We further show that gravitational anomalies cause cancellations between black hole contributions, removing the leading cusp divergence except at $c_L-c_R=0,\pm24$ in pure gravity and $c_L-c_R=0,\pm12$ in $\mathcal N=(1,1)$ supergravity. We also derive the spin structure dependent anomaly phases responsible for these cancellations.

</details>


### [4] [Analytic Bootstrap Solution for Quantum Mechanics on a Circle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36031)
*David Berenstein,Upamanyu Moitra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 作者提出用量子力学 bootstrap 方法求解圆周上粒子的薛定谔方程，利用测度傅里叶系数递归关系需指数衰减这一物理条件，得到解的（广义）连分数表示，并据此直接计算态密度、重建波函数及提取准动量，最后以 Mathieu 方程为例验证该方法能轻易分辨能带谱中指数级微小的能带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理情形下，需要求解测度傅里叶系数的递归关系，且这些系数必须指数级快速衰减；传统方法在处理此类问题时往往难以直接满足物理边界条件或难以刻画微小能带结构，因此需要一种既解析又能自然嵌入物理约束的求解途径。

Method: 采用量子力学 bootstrap 方法：将圆周上粒子的测度展为傅里叶级数，利用递归关系，并要求系数指数衰减，从而得到解的（广义）连分数表示；该解是耦合常数与能量的解析函数。随后直接从该表示计算态密度，将答案的物理性检验约化为对单个变量的不等式检验，并给出重建波函数与提取准动量随能量变化的方法。

Result: 解的（广义）连分数表示是耦合与能量的解析函数；态密度可直接计算，物理性判断被简化为单变量不等式；波函数可被重建，准动量可作为能量的函数提取。在 Mathieu 方程的最简单情形中，该方法能轻易分辨能带谱中指数级微小的能带。

Conclusion: 该 bootstrap 方法为圆周上的量子力学问题提供了一种解析、高效且物理约束自然的求解框架，能直接获得态密度、波函数和准动量，并在处理指数级微小能带等困难问题上表现出显著优势。

Abstract: We solve the Schrödinger equation for a particle in the circle with a quantum mechanical bootstrap method. The main idea is that in physical situations one needs solutions of the recursion relations for the Fourier coefficients of the measure that vanish exponentially fast. We show that this results in a (generalized) continued fraction representation of the solution. The solution is an analytical function of the couplings and the energy. We also show how one this method directly computes the density of states and checking that the answer is physical is reduced to checking an inequality for a single variable. We also show how to reconstruct the wave function and how to extract the quasimomentum of the particle as a function of the energy. We illustrate our solution in the simplest case of the Mathieu equation, where we show that the method can easily resolve exponentially small bands in the band spectrum.

</details>


### [5] [Cosmology of the Baby Universe in Coupled SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36032)
*Andrew Sontag*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了耦合 SYK 模型所实现的 AS² 几何中出现的“婴儿宇宙”结构，发展了有限 N、p 下有效的 Majorana 迹组合论证，从而给出[1]中弦规则的有限 λ 完备化；发现热 AdS 相与 AS² 相之间在 β→∞ 极限下仍存在一阶相变，并通过对婴儿宇宙的洛伦兹时间演化得到余弦标度因子，证实了其大爆炸/大挤压的宇宙学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在耦合 SYK 实现 AS² 几何的框架下，婴儿宇宙的内部结构尚未被系统理解；同时，此前用于计算的弦规则仅在特定极限下成立，缺乏有限 N 和 p、因而有限 λ 情形下的严格工具，需要发展新的组合论证来加以完备化并探索相结构。

Method: 作者发展与利用关于多个 Majorana 费米子迹的组合论证，这些论证在有限 N 和 p 下依然成立，从而为[1]中的弦规则提供有限 λ 的完备化；在此基础上解析婴儿宇宙的结构，研究由重粒子插入质量和 SYK 与 Maldacena-Qi 耦合常数之比所参数化的相图，并进一步考察婴儿宇宙中的洛伦兹时间演化。

Result: 发现热 AdS 相与 AS² 相之间存在一阶相变，且该相变在 β→∞ 极限下依然持续；相变由重粒子插入质量及 SYK 与 Maldacena-Qi 耦合常数之比两个参数刻画。对婴儿宇宙的洛伦兹时间演化分析得到余弦型标度因子，表明其体宇宙学具有大爆炸/大挤压（big bang / big crunch）的特征。

Conclusion: 通过有限 N、p 下有效的组合工具，作者完成了弦规则的有限 λ 完备化，厘清了婴儿宇宙的热力学相结构与相变行为，并确认其洛伦兹时间演化呈现大爆炸/大挤压的宇宙学图景，为 AS² 几何背后的量子引力结构提供了更完整的理解。

Abstract: We analyze the structure of the baby universe which arises in the coupled SYK realization of the AS$^2$ geometry. To do this, we develop and leverage combinatorial arguments about traces of many Majoranas which remain valid at finite $N$ and $p$ and thus furnish the finite $λ$ completion of the chord rules found in [1]. We find that there is a first order phase transition which persists in the $β\rightarrow\infty$ limit between the thermal AdS and AS$^2$ phases parameterized by the mass of the heavy particle insertion and the ratio between the SYK and Maldacena-Qi coupling constants. We then explore Lorentzian time evolution in the baby universe and find a cosine scale factor confirming the big bang / big crunch nature of the bulk cosmology.

</details>


### [6] [BPS Chaos and Fortuity in Higgs Branches I: The Gepner Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36035)
*Micha Berkooz,Trivko Kukolj,Ohad Mamroud,Josef Seitz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究第二类弦论卡拉比-丘紧化中的BPS态，将其Higgs支描述为囊括量子力学（quiver quantum mechanics），并把态区分为多中心引力子配置对应的单调态与占绝大多数的纯Higgs偶然态，进而将这一划分与目标空间两种不同上同调对应，并用Berry/Chern曲率作为BPS混沌的探针，在Gepner模型中对曲率及其随机矩阵理论性质作了局域化计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多卡拉比-丘紧化中的BPS态可用quiver量子力学描述，其简并度与BPS黑洞相似，但只有少部分态能连续到Coulomb支并对应多中心超引力膜配置，作者希望精确区分这两类态，并寻找探测BPS混沌的天然物理量。

Method: 作者将多中心超引力配置对应的态识别为单调态（monotone states），将占绝大多数的纯Higgs态识别为偶然态（fortuitous states），并论证这一区分在数学上对应Higgs支目标空间的两种不同上同调；同时提出用Berry/Chern曲率作为BPS混沌的探针，并在Gepner模型中利用辅助二维CFT中的局域化技术计算曲率、其随机矩阵理论（RMT）性质及相对RMT的偏离。

Result: 确立了BPS态分为单调态与纯Higgs偶然态两类，前者可连续到Coulomb支并对应多中心超引力配置，后者集中在特定R荷上；该划分对应目标空间两种不同上同调；在Gepner模型中通过局域化技术算得Berry/Chern曲率及其随机矩阵理论性质与偏离。

Conclusion: BPS态可自然划分为单调态与纯Higgs偶然态，数学上对应两种不同上同调；Berry/Chern曲率是探测BPS混沌的天然工具，局域化计算揭示了其在Gepner模型中的RMT统计特征及系统性偏离。

Abstract: The BPS states in Calabi-Yau compactifications of type II string theory can sometimes be described using quiver quantum mechanics whose Higgs branch is a complete intersection manifold. Their degeneracy resembles that of BPS black holes. A small fraction of these states corresponds to multi-center supergravity brane configurations and can be continued to the (near-Higgs) Coulomb branch. We identify them as monotone states. The overwhelming majority of states, known as pure-Higgs states, are fortuitous states, which concentrate at a particular $R$-charge. This division has a precise mathematical meaning as two different types of cohomologies of the Higgs branch target space. We argue that a natural probe of BPS chaos in these models is the Berry/Chern curvature. In the case of the Gepner model, we use localization techniques in an auxiliary two-dimensional CFT to compute the curvature, its RMT properties, and deviations from it.

</details>


### [7] [Local Measures in Odd de Sitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36045)
*Guilherme L. Pimentel,Nicole Righi,Behrang Tafreshi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明奇数维 de Sitter 空间中的无质量理论在树图层次具有非常简单的测度——即晚期波泛函的模平方为局域泛函，所有波函数系数在分离位置处为零，并给出了任意点数的一般闭式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶数维 de Sitter 空间中无质量理论的波泛函通常是非局域的，且其模平方（测度）包含来自非局域波函数的奇异行为，这给计算带来困难。作者希望揭示在奇数维情形下，这些奇异性如何只进入相位而不影响模平方，从而极大地简化测度。

Method: 作者证明奇数维 de Sitter 无质量理论在树图层次的测度等于晚期波泛函的模平方，且为局域泛函；由此所有波函数系数在分离位置处为零。利用这一性质，可以写出任意点数的闭式公式，并在三至七维空间中对非线性 sigma 模型、Yang-Mills 理论和广义相对论三个例子进行了研究。

Result: 在奇数维 de Sitter 空间中，无质量理论树图层次的测度是局域的，波函数系数在分离位置处全部消失；偶数维非局域波函数中所呈现的通常奇异性在奇数维情形下只影响相位。最终得到了适用于任意点数的闭式公式。

Conclusion: 奇数维 de Sitter 空间中的无质量理论具有显著简化的树图测度结构，其奇异性仅体现在相位中，使模平方成为局域泛函，从而能够写出任意点数的通用闭式公式，并在三个典型理论中得以验证。

Abstract: We show that massless theories in odd-dimensional de Sitter space have very simple measures---the modulus squared of the late-time wavefunctional---at tree level. They are local functionals; in particular, all wavefunction coefficients vanish at separated positions. All the usual singularities of nonlocal wavefunctions in even dimensions only affect the phase in the odd dimensional case. Hence a closed formula can be written for any number of points. We focus on three examples: the non-linear sigma model, Yang-Mills theory and General Relativity, from dimensions three to seven.

</details>

*Suzanne Bintanja,Amey P. Gaikwad,Dikshant Rathore*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 D1-D5 系统在 AdS₃×S³×T⁴ 上的 1/4-BPS 算符，在 Sym^N(T⁴) 周围做共形微扰论，精确到 N 地计算低阶扇区的反常维度，并发现其结果与超引力点的 BPS 态密度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解 D1-D5 系统（AdS₃×S³×T⁴）中 1/4-BPS 算符在有限 N 下的谱结构，特别是检验短多重态重组为长多重态时反常维度的具体形式，以及确认并非所有可能被抬升的态都会真正被抬升。

Method: 在 Sym^N(T⁴) 轨道折叠理论附近做共形微扰论（conformal perturbation theory），对低阶扇区的 1/4-BPS 算符精确到 N（即对任意 N 而非只在 N→∞ 极限）计算一阶非平凡变形下的反常维度。

Result: 所考虑扇区的所有反常维度都与短多重态重组为长多重态一致，且形式特别简单；在有限 N 及变形的一阶非平凡阶，所考虑扇区的 BPS 态密度与超引力点吻合，表明并非所有可被抬升的态都真的被抬升。

Conclusion: 在本文考虑的共形微扰论阶数下，有限 N 的 BPS 态密度与超引力点一致，证实谱的（未）抬升行为存在选择性，反常维度呈现出与多重态重组相容的简单形式。

Abstract: We consider the 1/4-BPS operators in the D1-D5 system on AdS$_3\times S^3\times T^4$. We evaluate the anomalous dimensions of low-lying sectors in conformal perturbation theory around Sym$^N(T^4)$ exactly in $N$. All anomalous dimensions are consistent with recombination of the short multiplets into long ones and take a particularly simple form. We show that at finite $N$ and at first nontrivial order in the deformation, for the sectors we consider, the BPS density of states agrees with that of the supergravity point. To the order in conformal perturbation theory we consider, this confirms that not all states that can get lifted do get lifted.

</details>


### [9] [Axionic Quintessence in Heterotic String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36064)
*David Andriot,Margherita Putti,Leo Veissiere*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在杂化弦紧致化框架下研究轴子型精质（axionic quintessence），从十维理论导出四维轴子有效作用量，考察超轻轴子产生的几何要求，并分析由此得到的精质势及其宇宙学解。


<details>

Motivation: 寻求弦论中能解释暗能量（精质）的候选粒子，重点探索杂化弦紧致化中由世界面瞬子指数压低产生的超轻轴子是否可行，并验证其与规范动能函数正定性等理论约束的相容性。

Aligned worldsheet-instanton contributions provide a way to alleviate this tension and yield an ultralight axion with a phenomenologically relevant decay constant. The framework is then extended to the quantum model, where we study the emergence of slow dynamics and the role of quantum correlations through Euclidean and real-time correlation functions. Finally, we explore how concepts such as replica symmetry breaking and disorder naturally arise in cosmological and holographic systems related to black hole fragmentation, highlighting spin glass models as useful theoretical laboratories for these phenomena. Abstract: We ask when tree-level supersymmetric cohomology admits a Hironaka decomposition: a unique expansion in finitely many secondary operators with polynomial coefficients in algebraically independent bosonic primaries. Such a decomposition organizes the cohomology into towers with a common set of unrestricted dressings. For diagonal models with $N$ labels, $d\geq1$ bosonic species and $f$ fermionic species, we construct this decomposition and obtain $(N!)^{d-1}2^{fN}$ secondaries. We also prove that the scalar-branch ring of ABJM theory admits a Hironaka decomposition at every rank and positive integer Chern--Simons level, and construct explicit bases for the $SU(2)\times SU(2)$ Klebanov--Witten scalar ring. We also do the same for the Higgs branches of two class-S theories. However, not all supersymmetric cohomologies admit a Hironaka decomposition. Abstract: The Disordered Universe is a novel research direction that exploits ideas and techniques coming from apparently unrelated research areas, ranging from cosmology to scattering amplitudes in particle physics and disordered complex systems, with the aim of addressing open questions in cosmology at all scales, from the very infra-red (or very late time) to the early universe. This paper acts as a general introduction and vision for a set of lectures delivered at the first La Ricotta Summer School "The Disordered Universe 2025", held from 21 July to 10 August 2025. These lectures involved open quantum systems, analytic properties of scattering amplitudes and correlation functions, foundations and quantum aspects of the de Sitter universe, as well as statistical physics out of equilibrium and spin glasses. Abstract: Modeling the gravitational waves from merging black holes requires precise calculations of the two-body problem. The divergence is known to come from periods of a K3 surface, which has a node at this speed. We show that the node lies on the integration contour of the K3 maximal cut, which makes this speed a Landau singularity of the cut. Requiring the divergence to cancel, without introducing new infinities, has been shown to fix the memory contribution, named for the permanent displacement that the waves leave in test masses, up to one constant. We derive the leading coefficients of its boundary integrals in closed form. We obtain both results from maximal cuts and boundary limits, without an integration-by-parts reduction of the full four-loop integrand. Abstract: We develop a quantum hydrodynamic theory based on Nambu's unpublished note \emph{A New Look at Fluid Dynamics}. The leading quadratic decay of quantum coherence is determined by the prepared quantum state. For two shear flows, we identify an exact noiseless sign subsystem. Introducing additional generating flows cancels the initial residual of the Euler equations while producing nonzero connection curvature, governed by the same kinetic integrals that determine the classical correction. At fixed mean energy, choosing amplitudes adapted to the dominant coupling flow substantially reduces the initial decoherence, even along the direction that cancels the Euler residual. This advantage gradually diminishes under conditional evolution, eventually leading to a crossing of the decoherence curves. Our example shows that a preparation with a smaller initial coherence-decay coefficient can have a larger maximum sampled loss over a finite readout interval.

Satisfying WZ consistency, and equipped with a variational principle for $\varphi$ that determines the conformal transformation $g_{μν}\!\to\!g_{μν}e^{-\varphi}$ to an associated spacetime with no anomaly, the WZ action resolves the conformal gauge fixing ambiguity of earlier methods of `integrating the anomaly.' In $D\!=\!2$ the WZ action is the local form of the Polyakov action, whose massless $\varphi$ propagator describes a correlated two-particle scalar intermediate state in the SET polarization function $\langle TT\rangle$. The imaginary part of this polarization obeys a UV finite sum rule, becoming a Dirac $δ$-function at zero energy for conformal quantum fields, demonstrating the infrared origin of the anomaly, while the real part becomes the light cone propagator of $\varphi$. Although the $\Box R$ term in $D\!=\!4$ corresponds to a cohomologically trivial cocycle, it also arises from the IR limit of massless quantum fields, independently of UV physics, and contributes to the WZ action for low energy gravity. Analogous to the WZ action for pseudo-Goldstone mesons in low energy QCD from the axial anomaly, the WZ action for the conformal anomaly provides a Nambu-Goldstone scalar field for macroscopic gravity, in addition to the metric of classical general relativity.

When the zero modes couple linearly and without derivatives to pairs of tower modes, $I_n$ falls as $1/m_n$, where $m_n$ is the mass of level $n$. In the cubic theory, this tail is independent of the ultraviolet cutoff, with coefficient $(μR)^2/1024π^3$ for compactification radius $R$, and the mutual information with the whole tower grows logarithmically with the number of levels. In the quartic theory, the tail is masked by states containing additional hard quanta, which make $I_n$ depend on the cutoff. Compared with the corrections the tower makes to low-energy amplitudes, which are dominated by the lightest levels and captured by local operators, the mutual information with the tower is not dominated by any finite set of levels and arises from the time-nonlocal part of the effective action. The local four-dimensional effective theory therefore describes the low-energy observables of the reduced system but not its state. Unbroken supersymmetry cancels the vacuum energy of the compactification but not the mutual information, which at leading order is a sum of squared amplitudes.

Finally we show that derivatives with respect to one of the path parameter generate numerators that together with the Parke-Taylor denominator build scattering amplitudes. Lorentz invariance fixes the spins of the inserted fields and the homogeneities of the corresponding twistor functions without any use of supersymmetry. The three-point outputs reproduce the kinematic forms of the light-cone cubic vertices of chiral higher-spin theory, and the derivative monomials of order four generate the components of the $\overline{\rm MHV}$ supermultiplet of $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory, off shell and on shell.

In these lectures, we review some elementary ideas of the de Sitter physics, passing through the representation theory of the de Sitter group, aspects of canonical quantum gravity, all the way to attempts in describing quasi-local features. Throughout, we highlight open problems that motivate ongoing efforts to understand quantum gravity and cosmology in universes with $Λ>0$. The results are relevant to non-Abelian gauge condensates in early-universe cosmology, including inflation, dark energy, and primordial gravitational waves, and to QCD-like gauge sectors above confinement where thermal effects are subleading.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Buice-Cowan 场论的连续极限假定神经元是欧氏空间中的点、活动按普通扩散传播，这既不能刻画皮层网络的层级组织，也不能刻画其小世界性质。作者希望构建一个保留皮层层级结构的场论框架，以正确描述此类网络的涨落、临界行为及小世界特性。

Conclusion: 决定平均场指数是否成立的是耦合沿层级的衰减方式，而非空间维度。p-adic/层级框架自然给出小世界属性，为在神经网络的层级组织与临界涨落之间建立定量联系提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: The Buice-Cowan field theory [PRE 75, 051919 (2007)] describes fluctuations and correlations of neural activity through a path integral whose mean-field limit is the Wilson-Cowan equation and whose critical behavior belongs to the directed percolation class. In its continuum limit the neurons are the points of a Euclidean space and activity spreads by ordinary diffusion, which encodes neither the hierarchical organization of cortical networks nor their small-world property. We formulate a version of the theory in which the neurons are organized in an infinite, self-similar, tree-like structure, described by the p-adic numbers. The path integral carries over unchanged, and finite networks are exact restrictions of the continuum theory. The main change is in the linear response: in the Euclidean theory every short-range kernel reduces to the Laplacian, while in the p-adic theory each connectivity kernel gives its own nonlocal p-adic diffusion operator. For couplings that decay by a fixed factor per level of the hierarchy this is the Taibleson-Vladimirov operator, and the critical theory is an ultrametric Reggeon field theory whose upper critical dimension is twice the decay exponent of the coupling. Hence the decay of the coupling up the hierarchy, and not the dimension of space, decides whether mean-field exponents apply. We obtain a Ginzburg-type criterion, loop-corrected hierarchical Wilson-Cowan equations, log-periodic corrections to scaling and power-law correlations. Exact simulations on a tree support the predictions: the avalanche-size exponent takes the mean-field value 3/2 when the coupling decays slowly and decreases toward the directed-percolation value when it decays fast. The hierarchical network is also small-world when the decay exponent is smaller than the dimension, a property that the Euclidean theory obtains only by assuming long-range coupling.

</details>

Method: 作者在 Buice–Cowan 场论的 p-adic 版本中，采用按层级以 p^{-(N+σ)} 衰减的耦合，该耦合自然挑选出 Taibleson–Vladimirov 算子；由此得到临界理论为超度量有向渗流，其上临界维度为 N_c=2σ。并在树上进行精确数值模拟。

Result: 数值模拟表明：当 σ≤N/2 时，雪崩尺寸指数 τ_S=3/2；当 σ>N/2 时，该指数连续下降。层级连接移除了对维度 N≥4 的限制。

Conclusion: 层级（p-adic）连接结构使得平均场临界分支行为（τ_S=3/2）在任意维度下均可实现，上临界维度由耦合衰减指数 σ 决定，突破了传统短程耦合的维度约束。

Abstract: Neural avalanches with size exponent $3/2$ are usually explained by mean-field critical branching, which for short-range coupling requires dimension $N\ge4$. We show that hierarchical connectivity removes this constraint. In a $p$-adic version of the Buice--Cowan field theory, a coupling that decays by $p^{-(N+σ)}$ per hierarchical level selects the Taibleson--Vladimirov operator, and the critical theory is ultrametric directed percolation with upper critical dimension $N_c=2σ$. Exact simulations on a tree give $τ_S=3/2$ for $σ\le N/2$, decreasing continuously for $σ>N/2$.

</details>

Conclusion: "原子即智能体"(AaaA)原理提供了一种完全数据驱动的方法，使宏观序可以从微观观测中自动涌现，为“机器学习物理学”开辟了新途径，并表明宏观序参量的涌现与语言/符号的涌现具有共同的集体预测编码机制。

Abstract: What is the intrinsic reason of emergence of macroscopic order parameter out of microscopic systems? In this article, we apply to physics the collective predictive coding (CPC), the theory of language/symbol emergence. As our language emerges out of our human communications, physical macroscopic order parameter emerges out of communications among atoms --- each atom is regarded as an agent who predicts each other's behavior through inference. We present three examples of classical spin systems: two-dimensional ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Ising models, a two-dimensional spin model with competing stripe orders, and the fully connected Sherrington--Kirkpatrick spin-glass model. In all of these examples, proper macroscopic order parameters including the replica overlap emerge automatically through communication informatics of CPC, only from observed spin configurations. We call the learned macroscopic coordinate the collective predictive order. Furthermore, for the ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Ising models, the equilibrium distribution of the collective predictive order yields an emergent potential with the symmetry and thermal evolution characteristic of a Landau potential. Notably, the emergence of the collective predictive order does not refer to any structure of microscopic Hamiltonians/Lagrangians. Thus our ``Atom-as-an-Agent" (AaaA) principle offers a completely data-driven method for the emergence of macroscopic order, that is, machine learning physics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 成功建立了VGLM的过拟合理论，解决了高维序参数带来的新数学挑战，并在多个典型VGLM回归模型上应用验证了该理论的正确性与价值。

Conclusion: GLM过拟合理论可推广到VGLM，尽管会增加数学复杂性但仍可用副本方法求解；该理论为后基因组医学推断和数据分析等易受过拟合困扰的领域开辟了新的应用前景。

Abstract: We show how the successful recent statistical physics based theory of overfitting in Generalized Linear Models (GLMs) can be generalized to a much larger family of regression models: the so-called Vector Generalized Linear Models (VGLMs), in which not a single linear combination of covariates drives the outcome, but where one has an arbitrary finite number of such linear combinations. The latter more general and complex models can describe more sophisticated statistical patterns in data, but are even more prone to overfitting than GLMs due to their significantly larger number of parameters; this often limits their use. We show that the generalization of the overfitting theory from GLMs to VGLMs causes many mathematical challenges that were absent from the earlier work, mostly related to the increased dimensional complexity of all order parameters in the theory, but that the new problem is once more solvable using the replica method. This opens up the field to many novel and welcome applications, especially in post-genome medical inference and data analysis, where overfitting is and remains a major barrier to progress. After developing the general VGLM overfitting theory, within the replica symmetric (RS) ansatz, we illustrate its merits and its correctness via application to several relevant VGLM regression models.

</details>


### [43] [Perfect sampling of Ising spin glasses with bounding chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37300)
*Lukas Péron,Louise Budzynski*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究基于"从过去耦合"(coupling from the past)与边界链(bounding chains)的伊辛自旋玻璃完美采样算法，在多种图族与耦合分布上分析其收敛（coalescence）行为，并给出理论与数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋玻璃由于存在高度非凸能量地形与复杂的耦合结构，传统的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法难以保证采样精确性；完美采样(perfect sampling)能提供无偏样本，但现有边界链方法在稀疏随机图、稠密图与全连接模型上的收敛速度与适用范围尚不明确，亟需理论刻画与算法改进。

Method: 采用耦合从过去(CFTP)结合边界链的框架。在Dobrushin条件下证明单点边界链在O(n log n)次更新内合并；对Erdős-Rényi随机图G(n,d/n)且耦合为标准高斯的情形，引入块(block)边界链，并将其合并时间证明为O(n log n)次块更新（每次块更新O(n)操作），该证明改编自Efthymiou与Zampetakis(2024)关于块Glauber动力学快速混合的结果；同时在稀疏随机图、周期格点与Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型上进行数值实验考察合并行为。

Result: 单点边界链在Dobrushin条件下以O(n log n)更新合并；稀疏随机图上数值表明，高效采样在整个树唯一性(tree-uniqueness)区域内成立，超过该阈值后变慢；在G(n,d/n)上，块边界链在树唯一性区域（d足够大）以O(n log n)块更新合并；周期格点上实验显示单点边界链受限于边界包络(looseness of bounding envelope)的松弛性；在Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型上，合并缓慢且在所有测试的倒温度下伴随损伤扩散(damage spreading)。

Conclusion: 树唯一性区域是完美采样可高效进行的核心阈值，块边界链在稠密随机图上比单点边界链更具优势；周期格点与SK模型的实验揭示现有边界表示的内在局限（包络过松、损伤扩散），因此提出替代的边界表示与耦合策略作为改进自旋玻璃完美采样的方向。

Abstract: We study perfect sampling algorithms for Ising spin glasses based on coupling from the past and bounding chains, on several graph families and coupling distributions. For the single-site bounding chain, we establish coalescence in $O(n\log n)$ updates under Dobrushin conditions. On sparse random graphs, our numerical results suggest that efficient sampling extends throughout the tree-uniqueness regime, with a slowdown beyond this threshold. On Erdős-Rényi random graphs $\mathcal{G}(n,d/n)$ with standard Gaussian couplings, we introduce a block bounding chain and prove its coalescence in $O(n\log n)$ block updates in the tree-uniqueness regime for sufficiently large $d$, each block update requiring $O(n)$ operations, adapting a proof in~\cite{Efthymiou_Zampetakis_2024} for the rapid mixing of block Glauber dynamics. On periodic lattices, our experiments instead suggest that the single-site bounding chain is limited by the looseness of its bounding envelope, whereas on the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model, slow coalescence is accompanied by damage spreading at every inverse temperature tested. These observations motivate alternative bounding representations and couplings as strategies to improve perfect sampling on spin glasses.

</details>


### [44] [Chaos picture of spin glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37426)
*Wenlong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出"混沌图景"(chaos picture)作为一种新颖、自洽且直观的自旋玻璃理论框架，旨在调和持续40年之久的"液滴模型 vs. 副本对称破缺"之争。该框架通过钉扎与随机液滴的动态筛选形成层级能量景观，揭示温度下降时实空间中尺度依赖液滴的渐进冻结，及其在相空间中的层级自由能景观分岔过程，并由此解释老化、年轻化与记忆效应等动力学现象，同时简化了对复杂亚稳态框架的再诠释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋玻璃领域长期存在两派对立的图像：液滴标度理论(droplet theory)与副本对称破缺(RSB, replica symmetry breaking)理论，二者争论持续40年而缺乏统合。本文旨在提出一个自洽且直观的新理论框架，以调和这一长期分歧，并同时解释自旋玻璃中多样的静态结构与动力学现象（老化、年轻化、记忆效应等）。

Method: 本文提出"混沌图景"框架。其核心机制包括：(1) 通过钉扎(pinning)与随机液滴的动态过滤(dynamic filtering)机制，构建层级化的能量景观；(2) 随着温度降低，实空间中尺度依赖的液滴发生渐进式冻结，这一过程对应相空间中层级自由能景观的分岔/碎裂过程；(3) 该分岔过程伴随温度混沌(temperature chaos)，从而在不违背液滴标度律的前提下实现动态纯态共存；(4) 界面几何本身并非关键机制，因此框架能自然涵盖界面从平面(planar)到分形(fractal)再到空间填充(space-filling)的交叉，与平均场极限相吻合。

Result: 混沌图景成功统合了液滴理论与副本对称破缺两大框架：温度下降时尺度依赖液滴的渐进冻结与相空间自由能景观的分岔过程一一对应，并通过温度混沌实现动态纯态共存而不违反液滴标度律。该框架还自然融合了随维度增加而发生的界面几何交叉（平面→分形→空间填充），与平均场极限一致，并对老化、年轻化、记忆效应等自旋玻璃动力学现象提供了深刻解释。

Conclusion: 混沌图景为自旋玻璃提供了一个统一、自洽且直观的理论框架，有效调和了40年的液滴模型与副本对称破缺之争，不仅统一了静态结构与动力学描述，还为复杂亚稳态框架提供了更简洁的重释，并有望对自旋玻璃实验与理论中的诸多疑难现象给出系统性理解。

Abstract: The chaos picture is a novel, coherent, and intuitive theoretical framework for spin glasses, reconciling the 40-year-old droplet vs. replica symmetry breaking debate. First, we argue the formation of a hierarchical energy landscape by pinning and dynamic filtering of random droplets. As the temperature decreases, the chaos picture describes a progressive freezing of scale-dependent droplets in real space, which mirrors a hierarchical free energy landscape fracturing process in phase space. This bifurcation process is accompanied by temperature chaos, leading to dynamic pure state coexistence without violating the droplet scaling. Crucially, the interface geometry is not critical to these mechanisms, naturally incorporating the interface crossover from planar, fractal, to space-filling as the dimensionality increases, in line with the mean-field limit. Finally, the chaos picture provides profound insights into spin-glass dynamics such as aging, rejuvenation, and memory effect, while offering a streamlined reinterpretation of the complex metastate framework.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过计算自旋分辨的局域场因子（local field factor），证明交换（exchange）单独给出的、恰好位于 2kF 以下的尖锐峰，在纳入平行自旋相关的动力学屏蔽相互作用最低阶图后约被消除一半，从而与量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）数据吻合；这两类图正对应 Hedin 顶点方程核的两个项，其中第二项通常被忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一个长期矛盾：仅考虑交换作用时，电子气的局域场因子在略低于 2kF 处出现尖锐峰值，但量子蒙特卡洛计算并未发现该峰。需要查明缺失的物理机制，并说明为何常用的近似会失效。

Method: 对两个自旋通道分别计算其最低阶费曼图，采用动力学屏蔽相互作用（dynamically screened interaction），且不引入任何拟合参数。将平行自旋相关项视为交换项的"负像"，并指出这两类图恰好构成 Hedin 顶点方程（vertex equation）核的两个项——其中第二项在常规计算中通常被丢弃。

Result: 平行自旋相关项约消除交换峰的一半；所得自旋分辨局域场因子在 rs=1 和 rs=2 下与 QMC 数据吻合，在 q=0 处与由基态能量得到的压缩率（compressibility）和自旋磁化率（spin susceptibility）一致。

Conclusion: 欲正确描述电子气的局域场因子，必须保留 Hedin 顶点方程核的第二项（即被routine 丢弃的项）；交换与平行自旋相关的组合给出了与 QMC 及各热力学量一致的、无拟合的描述。

Abstract: Exchange alone gives the electron gas a local field factor with a sharp peak just below 2kF. Quantum Monte Carlo finds none. Both spin channels are computed here from their lowest diagrams with a dynamically screened interaction and no fitting. Parallel spin correlation is the negative image of exchange and removes about half the peak; the calculated spin-resolved factors account for the QMC data at rs = 1 and 2, and at q = 0 for the compressibility and spin susceptibility obtained from the ground-state energy. The two classes of diagrams are the two terms of the kernel of Hedin's vertex equation, of which the second is routinely dropped.

</details>

Abstract: We establish an experimentally accessible route to identifying Majorana surface states through their intrinsic Ising spin response, using UTe$_2$ as a concrete platform. The key is to extend the Majorana correlations into an adjacent metallic film via the superconducting proximity effect, thereby making the otherwise surface-localized response accessible to NMR measurements. Remarkably, disorder further sharpens the Majorana signature: impurity scattering suppresses the conventional $p$-wave proximity correlations while preserving the Majorana contribution, yielding a giant Ising-anisotropic enhancement of the NMR relaxation rate. This approach also offers a powerful means to distinguish among candidate pairing symmetries.

</details>


### [47] [Determination of the Magnetic Penetration Depth from Finite-Element Susceptibility Calculations of Spheroidal and Cylindrical Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36133)
*Morgan Grant,Antony Carrington*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过有限元分析计算三维超导球体和圆柱体在有限穿透深度下的磁化率χ(λ)，量化形状相关的迈斯纳响应，提取λ→0极限下的有效退磁因子，并提出改进的解析表达式，为通过体积排除法精密测定λ提供高精度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁穿透深度λ是超导态的基本参数，通常由迈斯纳态磁化率χ的精密测量确定，但从χ中提取λ需准确处理几何相关的退磁效应，而解析结果仅在少数情形下可用。

Method: 采用三维有限元分析方法，对多种纵横比的超导球体与圆柱体计算磁矩和磁化率χ(λ)，在λ→0极限下提取有效退磁因子；对于小但有限的λ，由χ(λ)的初始变化确定有效样品尺寸，并据此提出改进的解析表达式。

Result: 量化了形状相关的迈斯纳响应，给出了宽范围纵横比下的有效退磁因子；确定了小λ情形下的有效样品尺寸，并提出改进的解析表达式用于描述χ(λ)的初始变化。

Conclusion: 研究结果为基于体积排除的磁化率测量确定λ提供了高精度流程，可直接应用于新型超导材料的表征。

Abstract: The magnetic penetration depth, $λ$, is a fundamental parameter of the superconducting state and is commonly determined from precision measurements of the magnetic susceptibility, $χ$, in the Meissner state. However, extracting $λ$ from such measurements requires an accurate treatment of geometry-dependent demagnetization effects, for which analytic results are only available in a limited number of cases. Here we present a finite-element analysis of the magnetic response of three dimensional superconducting spheroids and cylinders with finite penetration depth. By calculating the magnetic moment and susceptibility, $χ(λ)$, over a wide range of aspect ratios, we quantify the shape-dependent Meissner response and extract effective demagnetization factors in the $λ\rightarrow 0$ limit. For small but finite $λ$, we further determine effective sample dimensions from the initial variation of $χ(λ)$ and propose an improved analytical expression for these. Our results provide a high-precision procedure for determining $λ$ from volume-exclusion based susceptibility measurements and should be directly applicable to the characterization of novel superconducting materials.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 观测到峰值二极管效率33%的大SDE，出现在33.6 T高场下，为迄今最高场的SDE观测；非互易输运严格限于超导与场致自旋极化态共存、靠近一级变磁相边界的窄场域；远离该狭窄相交叉区则无SDE，排除了外在涡旋钉扎不对称；理论模型表明磁性序与三重态超导序的耦合可稳定奇宇称FFLO态。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明UTe₂中SDE源于自发反演对称破缺伴随有限动量库珀配对，而非外在涡旋钉扎效应；磁性与三重态超导序的耦合能够稳定奇宇称FFLO态，为等自旋配对超导体中有限动量配对及其非互易输运提供了新理解。

Abstract: The superconducting diode effect (SDE) is characterized by unequal critical current magnitudes for opposite-polarity current flow, owing to broken spatial-inversion and time-reversal symmetries. For a superconducting pair wavefunction to spontaneously break inversion symmetry implies the formation of Cooper pairs with nonzero momentum, yielding a nonuniform superconducting state. For singlet superconductors, the formation of such a (FFLO) state has long been understood to provide an escape from high field Pauli pair-breaking -- how and why such an effect might occur for equal spin pairing is an open question. Here we report the observation of a large SDE in bulk specimens of the three-dimensional heavy-fermion triplet superconductor UTe$_2$, with a peak diode efficiency of 33% at the high magnetic field strength of 33.6 T, representing the highest-field observation of the SDE to date. Crucially, this nonreciprocal transport is strictly confined to a narrow field regime where superconductivity coexists with a field-induced spin-polarized state near a first-order metamagnetic phase boundary. The absence of the SDE away from this slender phase-intersection argues strongly against purely extrinsic vortex pinning asymmetries. Instead, our findings indicate spontaneous inversion symmetry breaking accompanied by finite-momentum Cooper pairing. We model this behavior using a phenomenological Ginzburg-Landau theory, demonstrating that the intertwining of magnetic and triplet superconducting orders can stabilize an odd-parity FFLO state.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文针对4Hb-TaS₂实验中同时表现出的向列超导(Hc2各向异性)与手性超导(低温各向同性涡旋)这一矛盾现象，通过建立Ginzburg-Landau方程并开发高效数值算法求解涡旋解，发现应变使系统在转变点附近呈向列性、远离转变点则呈手性主导，从而解释了实验观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4Hb-TaS₂实验同时呈现出Hc2双重各向异性（暗示向列超导）与低温各向同性涡旋（暗示手性超导），这两种现象看似相互矛盾，需要理论解释这一"疑难"。

Method: 建立双分量序参数非常规超导体的Ginzburg-Landau方程，开发数值高效的涡旋求解算法以在宽参数范围内研究涡旋形状与各向异性；同时解析证明涡旋各向异性在向列相中对应变不敏感、在手性相中与应变成线性关系，从而为区分两相提供判据。

Result: 解析结果表明：向列情形下涡旋各向异性（至领头阶）与应变无关，而手性情形下与应变成线性依赖。数值与解析结合表明，在应变存在下，很大参数范围内系统在转变点附近表现为向列性（导致Hc2各向异性），而远离转变点则手性占主导（导致参数上更小的涡旋各向异性）。

Conclusion: 通过应变效应，系统可同时兼容向列与手性行为：近转变处向列性主导导致Hc2各向异性，远离转变处手性主导导致涡旋各向异性参数性减小，与4Hb-TaS₂实验观测一致。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductors with a two-component order parameter support the existence of a rotational symmetry breaking nematic phase and a time-reversal symmetry breaking chiral phase. A recent experiment on 4Hb-TaS$_2$ surprisingly shows both twofold anisotropy in $H_{c2}$, which suggests nematic superconductivity, as well as isotropic vortices at low temperatures, which suggest chiral superconductivity. To resolve this apparent conundrum we write down the appropriate Ginzburg-Landau equations and develop a numerically efficient algorithm for finding vortex solutions, allowing us to study their shape and anisotropy over a broad parameter range. We also demonstrate analytically that, to leading order, the vortex anisotropy is independent of strain in the nematic case, but is linearly dependent on it in the chiral phase, providing a possible way to distinguish the two phases. Using all these results we show that, in the presence of strain, in a large parameter regime the system behaves nematically near the transition, leading to an anisotropy of $H_{c2}$, but is dominantly chiral away from the transition, leading to parametrically smaller vortex anisotropy, in line with the experiment.

</details>

Abstract: To address the complex interplay of microstructural evolution, phase separation, superconductivity coherence and quantum fluctuations, we prepared Cuxwt%Nb series of granular samples by rapidly cooling, where the niobium concentration varied (x = 3, 5, 15, 20). The goal was to establish a systematic and robust method approach for obtaining samples with different of sub-micrometric precipitates distributions but also to investigate their superconducting properties. Structural characterization by optical and Scanning Electron Microscopy, dc Mdc[H,T] ac inductive , as well the magneto-transport measures rhodc[H,T], Rdc[V,I.H,T], I[V,H,T] and [H,T,I] were done. Also, using a micro Hall sensor with 10 measuring transverse voltage bridges regularly distributed, we were able to obtain the real external applied magnetic field value inside of the chamber sample space. Thus e studied the dependence of configuration of the Nb grains in the superconducting coupling order parameter properties that connects the grains superconductors to the normal Cu matrix. We verify through magnetization and transport measures the signature of the double transition. This, when we apply current of different intensities, in the transport measures, and different field values, in the inductive measures. This behavior is an indication that our system is made up of by a collection of superconducting grains immersed in a well-organized in a non-superconducting matrix. The results indicate that the superconducting properties of these systems are manifested usually through two transition temperatures Tc(H) e TcJ(H).

Abstract: We develop rotational perturbation theory for magnetic interactions in collinear and noncollinear systems, distinguishing prescribed rotations of exchange fields from constrained rotations of magnetic moments. At the full exchange splitting, we derive Green-function expressions for prescribedfield energy curvatures directly from perturbation theory, without invoking the local force theorem; constrained-moment curvatures additionally require the stationary magnetic response and are related to the inverse static susceptibility. We examine noncollinear pair interactions and planar and conical spin spirals, and derive the long-wavelength ferromagnetic spin-wave stiffness and, assuming adiabatic sublattice precession, the antiferromagnetic spin-wave velocity. Orbital-nonlocal magnetic potentials and correlated electronic response reveal further requirements for connecting rotational curvatures to collective magnetic behavior. As a practical extension, we project the complete transverse pair Hessian onto an effective scalar Heisenberg exchange and quantify the unreconstructed curvature.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study a frustrated seam produced by literal reflection gluing of Wen's plaquette model on a closed lattice Klein bottle. The seam operators anticommute along two cycles for even circumference and one cycle of twice the length for odd circumference. Their algebra reduces to free Majorana rings, while the commuting bulk stabilizers constrain the allowed ring sectors. This bulk locking yields exact ground energies, excitation gaps and degeneracies, checked by phase-aware Pauli traces and finite-size diagonalization, including two 24-qubit energy predictions. The seam degeneracy is not topological: the single-site seam fields tested split it at second order. We contrast this obstructed gluing with consistent toric-code realizations. An edge register preserves the anyon labels; a Hadamard-dressed site register has colour-dependent seam transport, with ground-state degeneracy 2 or 4 for even circumference and a sign-frustrated exception at odd circumference. For the enumerated lattice isometries and sizes, we compute logical gates and distances, distinguishing abstract qubit relabelling from local hardware depth. Finally, on three 16- and 24-qubit lattices, chiral matter selects a holonomy doublet whose electric-field splitting follows two competing tunnelling routes. These matter results remain finite-size statements. The main result is the exact obstructed-seam solution with bulk sector locking; the register, gate and matter calculations delimit its relation to topological codes.

</details>

Result: 发现虽然理想量子几何被认为有利于分数量子态，但偏离该极限可显著增强其稳健性，多体能隙比理想情况大一个数量级以上。无集中协调的智能体群自主探索并整合发现，表现优于使用相同工具的孤立智能体。AI 识别出实空间量子度量（而非动量空间度量）是决定多体能隙的关键量，揭示了平带波函数如何塑造双电子相互作用，并得到一个仅由单粒子波函数计算、无需多体精确对角化（ED）的指标来指导能隙优化。

Abstract: Can artificial intelligence (AI) move beyond solving well-defined problems to contribute to open-ended discovery in fundamental physics? Could AI propose what to explore, identify unexpected phenomena, and uncover their mechanisms? We address this using MOSAIC (Multi-agent Open-ended Scientific Analysis and Investigation through Collaboration), a collaborative swarm of AI agents (Claude Opus 5.5), to tackle a longstanding bottleneck: the small energy gaps limiting the robustness of fractional topological order and the study of anyons at higher temperatures. Rather than prescribing a model, we ask frontier models (GPT-6 Astra) to propose a theoretical framework, which MOSAIC explores through diverse strategies and rapid exchange of ideas. This reveals a striking finding: although ideal quantum geometry is considered favorable for fractional states, departing from this limit can dramatically enhance their robustness, with many-body gaps more than an order of magnitude larger than in the ideal case. Without centralized coordination, the agents explored autonomously, combined findings across agents, and formed insights from individual runs, outperforming isolated agents with the same tools. AI-assisted theoretical analysis further develops simplified models with an analytical gap enhancement factor that is unbounded within the single-band approximation. Remarkably, AI identifies the real-space quantum metric, rather than the momentum-space one, as the key quantity governing the many-body gap, revealing how flat-band wavefunctions shape two-electron interactions and yielding an index computable from single-particle wavefunctions without many-body ED to guide gap optimization. Our study demonstrates how collaborative AI agents can integrate exploration, discovery of unexpected physics, and theoretical understanding, opening new possibilities for AI-driven research in fundamental physics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异金属同时表现出电阻率随温度线性变化与比热 C/T 随冷却对数上升两种特征，但其背后的微观机制及二者的定量联系尚不明确。作者希望找到一个不依赖具体耦合常数的普适量，用来区分不同类型的临界散射机制。

Method: 作者考虑电子与一个局域临界浴耦合散射的模型，构造电阻率线性系数与比热对数系数之比 Q。由于耦合常数在比值中相互抵消，Q 仅由两个可观测量决定：临界浴的弛豫速率（通过光电子能谱或光学测量）和参与电流弛豫的临界熵份额。随后对共形浴（SYK 模型）、Yukawa–SYK 场和 Kondo 晶格等不同浴类别计算 Q 的取值区间。

Result: 计算得到：共形浴的 Q≤4.91（由 SYK 模型验证）；Yukawa–SYK 场的 Q 在 9–15 之间；Kondo 晶格的 Q 远小于上述值。实验上 LSCO 的 Q≈9，超过了共形浴的上界 4.91，因而指向 Yukawa–SYK 场。

Conclusion: Q 比值提供了一个区分奇异金属临界散射机制的无耦合常数诊断量。LSCO 的 Q≈9 超出共形浴界限，说明其奇异金属行为更符合 Yukawa–SYK 场机制。

Abstract: Strange metals pair a resistivity linear in temperature with a specific heat $C/T$ rising logarithmically on cooling. We find that, if electrons scatter off a local critical bath, the coupling cancels in the ratio $Q$ of their coefficients, leaving two measurable numbers: how slowly the bath relaxes, seen in photoemission or optics, and the share of critical entropy that relaxes current. Baths form classes: $Q\le4.91$ for a conformal bath, as SYK models confirm; 9--15 for the Yukawa--SYK field; and far less for Kondo lattices. LSCO, at $Q\approx9$, exceeds the conformal bound, pointing to the Yukawa--SYK field.

Within the present DFT+U+SOC description, the spectral response therefore depends on the separate Coulomb and exchange interactions and cannot be represented completely by a single effective U-J parameter. Abstract: Mobile anyons in topologically ordered phases provide a setting to investigate how fractional exchange statistics shapes collective behavior at finite density. We introduce AnyFormer: a neural variational framework for continuum anyons on a torus that explicitly incorporates statistical interactions and global winding constraints. We construct a smooth orbital frame that enforces the matrix-valued torus boundary conditions, and supplement it with an analytic pair factor that supplies the regular collision behavior. The orbital geometry and many-body amplitudes are optimized in parallel by variational Monte Carlo. We apply AnyFormer to semions without an external field or interaction potential, isolating unique correlations generated solely by statistics. Our framework closely matches exact two-semion energies and yields lower variational energies than finite-cutoff exact diagonalization for $N = 4$ and $6$ semions. Scaling up to $N = 16$ semions, well beyond the limits of continuum diagonalization, our calculations reveal states with pronounced correlation holes, suppressed long-wavelength density fluctuations, and a reversal of pair circulation at a distance set by the mean particle spacing. For $N = 12$ and $16$ semions, the circulation develops damped spatial oscillations. AnyFormer thus provides a scalable variational neural-net framework for microscopic studies of correlations, chiral motion, and collective phases of bulk anyonic matter.

  Recent studies suggest that magnetic moments
  coexist with tetramer distortions under pressure.

  The resulting model contains competing easy-plane
  single-ion and easy-axis exchange anisotropies.

  and evaluate the magnetic contribution to the lattice stiffness
  for several lattice distortion patterns including the tetramer pattern.
  We find that the tetramer pattern is the most favorable
  among the considered distortion patterns throughout the parameter range studied.
  Moreover, competition between the two anisotropies
  enhances the tendency toward lattice distortion
  and strengthens the preference for tetramerization over the other patterns.

Abstract: Super-Bloch oscillations (SBOs) offer a powerful route to control wave packet propagation in driven lattices, yet their realization in magnonic systems has remained elusive. Here, we predict SBOs of magnon wave packets in periodic magnonic crystals driven by static and ac gradients of magnetic anisotropy field. A static gradient alone induces magnon Bloch oscillation (BO) with a constant carrier frequency. Remarkably, adding a near-resonant MHz-scale ac driving gives rise to a slow large-amplitude envelope oscillation, the hallmark of SBO. At exact resonance, the ac driving enables phase-controlled directional transport with a carrier-frequency shift up to GHz scale. Consequently, the same structure can be switched among three distinct regimes: conventional BO, SBO, and directed transport. Analytical expressions for the oscillation amplitudes, superperiod, drift velocity, and frequency evolution are validated by micromagnetic simulations. Extending to antiferromagnet magnonic crystals, we further demonstrate that these effects drive opposite motions of two oppositely polarized magnon modes in THz range. Our work establishes magnon SBOs as a versatile platform, opening a new avenue for reconfigurable magnonic devices.

Abstract: In rare-earth compounds, the interplay between geometric frustration and unusual single-ion anisotropy caused by the crystal electric field often leads to exotic magnetic phases. Here, we investigate the magnetic properties of the rare-earth Shastry-Sutherland compound Er$_2$Be$_2$GeO$_7$ using bulk measurements and neutron scattering down to sub-Kelvin temperatures. Thermal- and cold-neutron inelastic scattering experiments demonstrate that the strong Ising anisotropy originates from the local crystal electric field, and reveal gapped magnetic excitations with weak dispersion in the ordered state. Mean-field random-phase approximation with inequivalent nearest-neighbor couplings accounts well for the collective excitations, while classical Monte Carlo simulations yield the fractional $1/4$ and $1/2$ magnetization plateaus. Our work provides a comprehensive description of the spin correlations in Er$_2$Be$_2$GeO$_7$ with a particular focus on the classical origin of its fractional magnetization plateaus.

Abstract: We theoretically analyze the spin-polarized tunneling between SmB$_6$ tip and the bicollinear antiferromagnet Fe$_{1+x}$Te. The emergence of magnetic contrast in tunneling requires time-reversal symmetry breaking on the surface of the tip. The magnetic contrast part of the tunneling conductance then has two contributions: the first one originates from the magnetic moment due to the equilibrium long-range order on the surface, while the other is governed by the magnetic moment induced by the electric field of the junction through the surface magneto-electric effect. We show that the ratio of the equilibrium moment to the response extracted near zero bias equals, up to the sign, twice the bias at which the contrast changes sign, independently of the relative strength of the two mechanisms, so that this ratio itself does not measure the strength of the magneto-electric effect. We find that within the experimental energy window $|eV|\lesssim10$~meV the {bias-dependent} field-induced contribution is at most a few percent of the equilibrium one, and the two become comparable only at biases of order $100$~meV or higher. The ratio of the field-induced to the equilibrium contribution is inversely proportional to the distance between the tip and the substrate.

Abstract: Classical Coulomb crystals and quantum Wigner crystals typically arise when repulsive interactions order like-charged particles in spatially confined environments. Here, we show that transient crystallization can also emerge in freely propagating electron pulses without external trapping. Experimentally, we use a transmission electron microscope to measure number-resolved energy correlations in photoemitted pulses containing up to nine electrons, revealing strong correlations at small particle numbers that progressively disappear in the pulse interior as the number increases. To explain these observations, we combine many-body simulations with a one-dimensional statistical-mechanical model, showing that the leading and trailing electrons generate a self-induced longitudinal potential that transiently confines the inner particles, enabling the formation of an ordered chain during propagation. The resulting crystallization phase diagram reveals how many-body ordering dynamics can be understood in terms of evolving macroscopic beam properties, namely its geometry and the ratio of the mean Coulomb interaction energy to the longitudinal thermal energy. Extending this framework, we theoretically demonstrate that weak transverse guiding through electrostatic or optical ponderomotive forces can sustain crystallization at larger particle numbers and predict spontaneous longitudinal periodic bunching in continuous electron beams. Our results identify conditions under which stochastic Coulomb interactions can be harnessed to generate self-structured, strongly correlated electron beams in both pulsed and continuous regimes.

</details>

Abstract: This thesis develops diagrammatic methods for thermodynamic and subsystem quantities of interacting lattice fermions, providing reference calculations for quantum simulators and many-body approximations. I formulate an inverse-flavour expansion about a self-consistent Hartree saddle, enumerate non-Gaussian diagrams, and use a discrete Lehmann representation to integrate selected internal vertices analytically. Density results cover 31 state points of the six-flavour Hubbard model on a $70\times70$ lattice at $T/t=0.15$.
  I generalize combinatorial summation to flavour-dependent propagators and expansion shifts. CoS++ combines partial contractions to obtain connected sums for interaction subsets. A determinant-polynomial sieve evaluates the same sums through local coefficient filters; at ninth order it is 24--25 times faster than the specified CoS baselines in three-flavour dense-random-matrix benchmarks. Thermodynamic applications include a 2048-site honeycomb lattice and a three-flavour square lattice with 1024 sites.
  For the second Renyi entropy, I derive a fermionic replica path integral and a stable evaluation of its two-time Green functions. The connected-determinant calculation reaches tenth order on a $3\times3$ cluster and ninth order on larger lattices. On $50\times50$ sites at $(U/t,μ/t,βt)=(3,1,8)$, the ninth-order continuation at shift $α=1.3$ gives $S_2/N_s=0.06695$ with propagated Monte Carlo uncertainty $3.0\times10^{-5}$. Abstract: A transition between an integer quantum Hall insulator and a chiral spin liquid can become critical without closing the single-electron gap, raising the question of which composite excitations govern the change in topological order. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: n=5 是 Lane–Emden 方程区分有限半径与无限半径自引力平衡态的临界多方指数，其上 Schuster 剖面产生的标度族诱导中性模，理解该临界情形的解结构、唯一性与稳定性是核心动机。

Method: 利用标度族 θ_λ(ξ)=λ^{1/2}θ(λξ) 及中性模 φ_0=½θ+ξθ'；应用 Schuster 变换与对数变量将方程化为自治哈密顿系统 ν̈=¼(ν−ν⁵) 并求一阶积分；用 Weierstrass ℘ 函数显式表示所有穿过 ν=0 的轨道并与数值积分比对（约 10⁻¹² 精度）；在同调不变量变量下分析奇异解 θ=(2ξ)^{−1/2} 在 n=5 处的中心稳定性，并对带位移平衡的距离变化 Emden–Fowler 系统证明平衡点线性与非线性不稳定；将 Schuster 剖面的线性化作薛定谔算子 L=−Δ_r−5θ_S⁴ 于 L²((0,∞),ξ²dξ)，取其 Friedrichs 扩张并分析谱。

Conclusion: 临界指数 n=5 下 Lane–Emden 方程具有清晰的轨道分类与封闭解结构，Schuster 剖面为中心正则的唯一解且为能量泛函的鞍点；其线性化薛定谔算子的谱特征（本质谱、零能阈值共振、唯一负特征值）与其过渡性临界稳定性一致。

Abstract: We study the Lane--Emden equation of index $n=5$, the critical polytropic case separating finite-radius from infinite-radius self-gravitating equilibria. The scaling family $θ_λ(ξ)=λ^{1/2}θ(λξ)$ generates a neutral mode $φ_0=\tfrac12θ+ξθ'$ about the Schuster profile. Schuster's transformation and a logarithmic variable reduce the equation to the autonomous Hamiltonian system $\ddotν=\tfrac14(ν-ν^5)$ with a first integral. Besides the Schuster (separatrix) and Srivastava solutions, every orbit crossing $ν=0$ is given in closed form by a Weierstrass $\wp$-function, and the closed forms agree with direct numerical integration to about $10^{-12}$. Up to scaling and sign, the Schuster solution is the only solution regular at the center, and it separates positive solutions from solutions with infinitely many zeros. In homology-invariant variables the singular solution $θ=(2ξ)^{-1/2}$ is a center exactly at $n=5$, where its stability changes. For a distance-varying Emden--Fowler system with a shifted equilibrium we prove that at $n=5$ the unique equilibrium is linearly and nonlinearly unstable. Finally, the linearization about the Schuster profile is the Schrödinger operator $L=-Δ_r-5θ_S^4$ on $L^2((0,\infty),ξ^2dξ)$. Its Friedrichs extension has essential spectrum $[0,\infty)$, a threshold resonance at $0$ carried by the non-normalizable scaling mode, and exactly one negative eigenvalue, $λ_0\approx-1.21$, so the Schuster profile is a saddle point of the associated energy functional.

</details>

Despite the nonzero jump, connected susceptibility is infinite both at criticality and in a neighboring ordered interval. At the critical coupling, the excess magnetization decays more slowly than any positive power of the field as the field tends to zero. Abstract: We present a self-contained formulation of longitudinal motion in one-dimensional second-gradient continua. Starting from finite-strain kinematics, we then apply the Principle of Virtual Power to identify the governing field equation and contact actions, derive the balance laws, and establish the mechanical-energy identity for hyperelastic materials. Motivated by pantographic microstructures, we study a class of finite-strain energies formulated in terms of normalized strain measures and deformation-dependent strain-gradient moduli. For the associated equation of motion, a traveling-wave first integral yields a turning-point criterion for the existence of solitary waves with positive stretch, exponential decay, and finite mechanical energy, as well as uniqueness at each fixed speed within a prescribed strain interval. For suitable spring energies, a change of strain variable reduces this integral to a polynomial equation, yielding explicit tensile and compressive profiles for Green--Lagrange and Hencky strains under appropriate parameter and kinematic conditions. Under the positivity condition on the quartic potential, the constructed Hencky energy has several physically natural properties: it is nonnegative, admits the natural state as its unique zero and global minimizer, has a positive linearized modulus, penalizes stretch gradients, and diverges under both material collapse and unlimited extension. Furthermore, the stored energy is coercive with respect to the $H^1$ norm of logarithmic strain. Together with mechanical-energy conservation, this yields uniform upper and positive lower bounds on stretch throughout the lifespan of smooth solutions. The theory's accessible mechanical origin, relatively simple mathematical foundation, and diverse solitary waves offer a tractable entry point to studying the rich nonlinear dispersive equations governing higher-gradient elastic continua.

</details>

Abstract: We study the many-body boson star equation in the mean-field regime. Furthermore, for the many-body dynamics, we establish both a maximal velocity bound, which can be seen as a Lieb--Robinson-type bound, and a minimal velocity bound. Abstract: In a recent paper~\cite{BGW}, Bizoń, Glogić, and Wasserman (BGW) reduced the classification of smooth self-similar Yang--Mills profiles in odd spatial dimensions $d\ge11$ to an algebraic problem of counting roots of an explicit polynomial in a prescribed interval. Two roots correspond to explicit profiles. BGW conjectured that there is exactly one additional root and verified this in a range of odd dimensions. Here, we prove the existence of an additional root, and hence of a third smooth self-similar profile, in every odd dimension $d\geq11$. The proof combines an endpoint sign calculation for the resonance polynomial with an algebraic argument showing that the third root is distinct from the two explicit ones. Uniqueness remains open in general.

</details>

Motivation: 动理学理论表明，经典相互作用粒子在适当标度极限下会呈现非平衡混沌（分子混沌）性质，现有分析通常局限于稀释区或短程势。本文旨在将这一框架同时推广到稀释与非稀释两个区域，并处理更一般的相互作用势，以刻画任意阶关联函数的衰减。

Conclusion: 该猜想成立，且无须额外的正则性假设。所得的迹界在最一般的非迹保持条件下依然成立，常数 d 不可改进（最优）。这一结果揭示了完全正或 2-正半群的迹增长与其极大、极小特征值实部之间的普适联系，并可理解为对衰减速率相对主导增长速率施加的普适约束。

Abstract: Vom Ende, Chruściński, Kimura and Muratore-Ginanneschi proved that every 2-positive trace-preserving map $Φ$ on $M_d=M_d(\mathbb{C})$ satisfies $\operatorname{Tr}Φ\le d\,m(Φ) + d^2 - d$, where $m(Φ)$ is the smallest real part of an eigenvalue of $Φ$, and deduced the universal relaxation-rate bound $\operatorname{Tr} L \le d\,m(L)$ for generators of 2-positive trace-preserving semigroups. They asked whether trace preservation is needed, and conjectured that every conditionally 2-positive map $L$, i.e. every $L$ for which $e^{tL}$ is 2-positive for all $t \ge 0$, satisfies $\operatorname{Tr} L \le d\,m(L) + (d^2 - d)\,s(L)$, where $s(L)$ is the largest real part of an eigenvalue of $L$. We prove this conjecture. The argument normalizes a 2-positive map by a positive definite eigenmatrix of its Hilbert-Schmidt adjoint, which turns it into a 2-positive trace-preserving map in the manner of a quantum Doob transform. The existence of such an eigenmatrix is guaranteed after adding an arbitrarily small multiple of the unnormalized depolarizing map, and the inequality then passes to the limit by continuity of the spectrum. No irreducibility, diagonalizability or faithful-invariant-state assumption is required. We also show that the constant $d$ is optimal within the class of non-trace-preserving completely positive semigroups, and we recast the result as a universal constraint on decay rates measured relative to the dominant spectral growth rate.

Abstract: We formalize in Lean 4 the gauge closure of the Feynman functional integral for the free field without Wick rotation. First, the restriction to box divisions is removed via a direction bound and a uniform Riemann-sum estimate for all $δ$-fine cylindrical divisions. Second, the coordinate measure on $\mathbb{R}^k$ is identified with the standard Gaussian one. Third, the Saks-Henstock lemma and additivity are proved. Fourth, the Fresnel formula $\int_{\mathbb{R}} e^{i a x^2/2} dx = \sqrt{2π/a} e^{iπ/4}$ is proved as an improper (gauge) integral. This yields the projective consistency of the oscillating Gaussian premeasure and the existence of the free-field integral. The free field is completely closed: we prove the Fresnel formula for an arbitrary symmetric positive-definite action $A$, compute the generating functional and the two-point function $\langle y_p y_q \rangle = i(A^{-1})_{pq}$, and prove Wick's theorem. The interacting field $λ\varphi^4$, unbounded generators, and renormalisation remain open. All results are machine-verified with no sorry or new axioms.

Abstract: Quantum field theory unifies special relativity and quantum mechanics, yet relies on externally imposed prescriptions for quantization, renormalization, and probabilistic interpretation that lack a common algebraic foundation. Abstract: We consider three-dimensional Bose gases with attractive two-body interactions and repulsive three-body interactions. TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理信息集成的（PIE）学习框架，将结构-性能关系、加工路线与可持续性标准相结合，用于机制驱动的合金设计。通过在镁合金上的验证，该框架从数百万候选合金中识别出两种兼顾性能、成本与碳排放的可持续合金方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续合金设计需同时兼顾高性能与原料稀缺性、工艺能耗、监管阈值及经济性等现实约束，而传统框架仅以性能为导向，缺乏对可持续生产路径的综合考量。

Method: 提出物理信息集成（PIE）学习框架，将基础结构-性能关系与加工路线、可持续性标准相结合；从成分-微观结构关系预测性能，并在可持续性约束下推断加工路线；以镁合金为对象，系统探索候选成分与微观结构，构建数百万种候选合金的数字库。

Result: 识别出两种可持续镁合金方案：含稀土合金在相近成本与排放下，屈服强度和抗拉强度较同族合金分别提升72.8%和61.1%；无稀土合金在力学性能不下降的前提下，成本降低40.4%、碳排放降低88.9%。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了一个通用、物理信息驱动且可量化的框架，用于在现实约束下设计可持续合金，突破了传统性能-成本-排放的权衡困境。

Abstract: Designing sustainable alloys must reconcile high performance with real-world constraints, including feedstock scarcity and variability, processing and energy requirements, regulatory thresholds, and economic limitations. Addressing these constraints reaches far beyond today's conventional performance-focused frameworks. It needs to consider not only complex property-processing-performance trade-offs but also sustainable production pathways. Here, we propose a physics-informed ensemble (PIE) learning framework that integrates fundamental structure-property relationships with processing routes and sustainability criteria for mechanism-based alloy design. It predicts properties from composition-microstructure relationships and infers processing routes under sustainability constraints, establishing an interdisciplinary approach to identifying sustainable alloy solutions. We validate the approach for Mg alloys, a lightweight yet energy-intensive metallic material class, considering performance, cost, and carbon emissions. Trained on a benchmark dataset, the PIE model systematically explores plausible alloy compositions and microstructures to construct a digital library of millions of candidate alloys. From this space, we identify two sustainable Mg alloy solutions that overcome the conventional performance-cost-emission trade-off: a rare-earth-containing alloy increases yield and tensile strength by 72.8 percent and 61.1 percent, respectively, over alloys from the same family at comparable cost and emissions; and a rare-earth-free alloy reduces cost and carbon emissions by 40.4 percent and 88.9 percent, respectively, relative to its primary-feedstock counterpart, without compromising mechanical performance. This work introduces a general, physics-informed, and quantifiable framework for designing sustainable alloys under real-world constraints.

</details>


### [87] [Amorphization-Depth-Dependent Bismuth Redistribution and Activation in Silicon for Quantum Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35993)
*Youcef A. Bioud,Simon-Jean Michel,Sebastien Francoeur,Sjoerd Roorda,Eva Dupont-Ferrier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文研究了20 keV铋离子注入硅中不同非晶化深度（直接注入约47 nm，预非晶化约177±15 nm）后的再分布与电激活行为，发现退火后替位铋比例相近（约90%），但霍尔电激活率差异显著（约60% vs 90%），且非晶/晶体界面的端区缺陷附近存在埋层铋俘获，表明非晶化深度与再生长几何结构主导铋的再分布并影响电激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近表面铋施主因丰富的电子-核自旋结构和长自旋相干时间而有望用于硅量子器件，但其电激活受离子注入引起的非晶化强烈影响，需要厘清非晶化深度与再生长过程如何调控铋的再分布和电激活。

Method: 对比两种工艺：20 keV Bi直接注入Si与SiO2预非晶化后注入；经650°C退火300 s后，采用卢瑟福背散射/沟道分析替位铋比例，霍尔测量电激活率，深度分辨离子散射分析埋层特征，辅以X射线光电子能谱与低温光致发光表征。

Result: 两种工艺非晶层深度分别约47 nm和177±15 nm；退火后替位铋比例相近（91±2% vs 89±4%），但霍尔电激活率分别约60%和90%；深度分辨分析在初始非晶/晶体界面附近的端区缺陷区发现埋层铋俘获特征，且更高退火温度下表面偏析加剧；纯Bi样品未检测到Bi氧化物贡献，并观测到施主相关光学信号。

Conclusion: 非晶化深度和再生长几何结构决定铋的再分布，并超越单纯的替位掺入而进一步影响电激活，这对硅量子器件中近表面铋施主的制备与激活具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Near-surface bismuth donors are promising for silicon quantum devices because of their rich electron-nuclear spin structure and long spin coherence, but their electrical activation is strongly influenced by implantation-induced amorphization. We compare redistribution and electrical activation of 20 keV Bi implanted directly into Si and after Si preamorphization. The two routes produce amorphous-layer depths of approximately 47 nm and 177 +/- 15 nm, respectively. After annealing at 650 C for 300 s, Rutherford backscattering spectrometry and channeling reveal comparable substitutional Bi fractions of 91 +/- 2% and 89 +/- 4%, whereas Hall measurements indicate Hall-derived electrical activation yields of approximately 60% and 90%, respectively. Depth-resolved ion-scattering analysis reveals a buried Bi-related feature at or beyond the initial amorphous/crystalline interface, within the end-of-range region, together with increasing surface segregation at higher annealing temperatures. The buried feature is consistent with trapping near residual end-of-range defects. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy and low-temperature photoluminescence on the Bi-only sample reveal no resolvable Bi-oxide contribution and a donor-related optical signature, respectively. These results show that amorphization depth and regrowth geometry govern Bi redistribution and influence electrical activation beyond substitutional incorporation alone.

</details>


### [88] [STEM-Bank-26: An Open Dataset of Analytical Scanning Transmission Electron Microscopy Images and Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36086)
*George Hollyer,Eric Stach*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发布 STEM-Bank-26 数据集：一个 929 GB、包含 37,921 张 STEM 图像的开放数据集，覆盖 1,095 个材料样品、1,499 个实验会话，其中 65.5% 的会话附带 EDS 或 EELS 光谱数据，并提供从归档数据构建同类数据集的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）是多模态、纳米尺度材料研究的重要手段，但跨多年、大规模公开的 STEM 数据集极为稀缺，制约了数据驱动的研究发展。

Method: 作者收集并整理了 STEM-Bank-26 数据集，涵盖 929 GB、37,921 张 STEM 图像，来自 1,095 个材料样品、1,499 个实验会话；并通过比对连续图像间的元数据提取显微镜控制轨迹，同时提供从归档数据构建类似数据集的工具。

Result: 构建了迄今最大的、整合实验 STEM 图像与 EDS/EELS 光谱数据（65.5% 会话含光谱）的开放数据集，并成功提取显微镜控制轨迹。

Conclusion: STEM-Bank-26 填补了大规模多模态 STEM 数据公开的空白，为纳米材料研究中基于数据驱动和机器学习的方法提供了关键资源与可复现的数据构建流程。

Abstract: Scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM) is a versatile, multi-modal method for studying materials at the nanoscale. It integrates multiple signal sources to provide atomic resolution imaging alongside spectroscopy for elemental composition and electronic structure. Despite its utility, few datasets spanning multiple years of data have been published. We introduce STEM-Bank-26, a 929 GB open dataset of 37,921 STEM images that spans 1,095 material samples across 1,499 sessions and includes energy-dispersive spectroscopy (EDS) or electron-energy loss spectroscopy (EELS) data for 65.5% of sessions. We extract microscope control trajectories by comparing metadata between sequential images and provide tools for building similar datasets from archival data. To our knowledge, this is the largest openly available dataset that integrates experimental STEM, EDS and EELS data across a broad distribution of samples and sessions.

</details>


### [89] [A Hybrid Brownian Dynamics-kinetic Monte Carlo Framework for Coupled Magnetic and Rotational Dynamics of Nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36102)
*Siraj Ul Haq,Iago López-Vázquez,Kazuya Okada,Sergiu Ruta,Amira Páez-Rodríguez,Lucía Gutiérrez,Òscar Iglesias,David Serantes,Roy W. Chantrell*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合布朗动力学—动力学蒙特卡洛（BD–kMC）框架，用于高效模拟悬浮磁性纳米粒子在低频交变磁场下磁矩与旋转的耦合动力学，并通过与随机 Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert（LLG）模拟对比验证其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实验相关时间尺度上模拟悬浮磁性纳米粒子（MNPs）的磁-旋转耦合动力学计算成本高昂，尤其在低频交变磁场下，磁矩的热激活翻转与布朗旋转的时间尺度分离使得传统方法难以兼顾精度与效率。

Method: 开发 BD–kMC 混合框架：将热激活磁切换交由 kMC 过程处理，同时保留磁矩的局部涨落。局部涨落通过 Metropolis 蒙特卡洛（MC）采样引入，使磁矩能偏离能量极小值，从而正确捕捉驱动粒子旋转的热平均各向异性力矩。通过引入势垒俘获判据，将 MC 试移动限制在当前能量阱内，以保证局部涨落与磁矩翻转之间的区分。

Result: 对非相互作用的单畴 MNP，与随机 LLG 模拟对比表明，BD–kMC 框架在平衡力矩和稳态易轴取向方面一致，包括正确再现外加磁场驱动下易轴由主要为垂直取向转变为主要平行取向的转变。

Conclusion: BD–kMC 框架为磁-布朗耦合动力学提供了一种高效方法，显著降低探索低频磁应用中长时间尺度所需的计算成本。

Abstract: Simulating the coupled magnetic and rotational dynamics of suspended magnetic nanoparticles (MNPs) over experimentally relevant timescales remains computationally demanding, particularly under low-frequency alternating magnetic fields. Here, we develop a hybrid Brownian Dynamics--kinetic Monte Carlo (BD--kMC) framework that combines thermally activated magnetic switching with particle rotation while retaining local fluctuations of the magnetic moment. These fluctuations are incorporated through Metropolis Monte Carlo (MC) sampling, allowing the moment to depart from the energy minima and thereby capturing the thermally averaged anisotropy torque that governs particle rotation. To preserve the distinction between local fluctuations and magnetic reversals, a barrier-trapping criterion confines MC trial moves to the current energy well, leaving thermally activated switching to the kMC process. For non-interacting single-domain MNPs, comparison with stochastic Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert (LLG) simulations demonstrates agreement in the equilibrium torque and stationary easy-axis orientation, including the field-driven transition from predominantly perpendicular to parallel alignment. The resulting BD--kMC framework provides an efficient approach to coupled magnetic and Brownian Dynamics, reducing the computational cost of exploring the long timescales relevant to low-frequency magnetic applications.

</details>


### [90] [Physical structure as an organizing principle for universal Hamiltonian learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36135)
*Mingkang Liu,Zhanghao Zhouyin,Guanghui Wang,Daye Zheng,Lei Shen,Qiangqiang Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: UniTB 是一个通用 E(3) 等变哈密顿量表示框架，融合密度泛函紧束缚理论的物理先验与深度学习，能跨化学体系泛化、保持下游性质物理合理，并以低成本支持大超胞计算与高通量材料筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFT 成本过高，难以用于大超胞计算与材料筛选；机器学习的哈密顿量模型虽能绕过自洽循环，但通常体系特定。需要一种通用模型，能跨化学组成与非晶固体泛化，同时保持下游性质物理合理并保持低计算成本。

Method: 提出 UniTB：一个通用 E(3) 等变哈密顿量表示框架。网络对先验哈密顿量（源于密度泛函紧束缚理论）进行修正，通过“由该先验路由的专家混合”实现跨化学泛化，并采用类似自洽电荷紧束缚的电荷平衡头。引入投影约束以限制先验带来的能带偏移，抑制伪态。提供最快 SO(2) 张量积 GPU 核。变体 UniTB-Flash 由双中心表以 O(N) 代价组装其先验（N 为原子数），优于网格上的 O(N log N)。

Result: 在 29,242 个晶体上训练，UniTB 对覆盖 68 种元素的非磁性测试晶体达到 0.48 meV 的矩阵元误差。在未使用非晶训练数据的情况下，两种模型对非晶 Ge-Sb-Te 的光学与电导率的 DFT 结果一致。UniTB-Flash 的候选排序与 UniTB 无显著差异，而 UniTB 的性质值更准确；二者联合筛选了 16,195 个晶体，覆盖五个工业相关应用领域。

Conclusion: UniTB 实现了通用且物理合理的哈密顿量预测，兼具高精度与低计算成本，适用于大超胞计算与大规模材料筛选，其 UniTB-Flash 变体进一步将先验组装降至 O(N) 成本，验证了在非晶与工业应用场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Density functional theory (DFT) gives the electronic structure of materials, but its cost limits large-supercell calculations and materials screening. Machine-learned Hamiltonians bypass the self-consistent cycle but are usually system-specific; universal ones could cover new compositions and disordered solids. Such models must generalize across chemistry and keep downstream properties physical, while staying inexpensive for large cells. Here we introduce UniTB, a universal E(3) equivariant Hamiltonian representation framework that integrates physical priors rooted in density-functional tight-binding theory with deep learning. UniTB's network, which corrects a prior Hamiltonian, generalizes across chemistry through a mixture of experts routed by that prior and a charge-equilibration head as in self-consistent-charge tight binding. A projection bounds band shifts from the prior, suppressing spurious states and keeping downstream properties physical. We provide the fastest SO(2) tensor-product GPU kernels for the network. The variant UniTB-Flash assembles its prior from two-center tables at $O(N)$ cost for $N$ atoms, against $O(N\log N)$ on a grid. Trained on 29,242 crystals, UniTB reaches a 0.48 meV matrix-element error on nonmagnetic test crystals spanning 68 elements. Without amorphous training data, both models agree with DFT optics and conductivity of amorphous Ge-Sb-Te. UniTB-Flash ranks candidates with no significant difference from UniTB, whose property values are more accurate; together they screen 16,195 crystals for five application domains of industrial relevance.

</details>


### [91] [Anisotropic Structural and Magnetic Response in Sm$_2$Ir$_8$Al$_{31}$: Al-Sublattice Reconstruction upon Sm Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36140)
*Mingyu Xu,Weiwei Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过用较大的4f钐原子替代UIr₄Al₁₅中较小的5f铀原子，作者合成了Sm₂Ir₈Al₃₁并解析了其超结构，发现这种化学压力引起各向异性的结构响应——Al亚晶格发生重构而非Ir-Al骨架均匀膨胀，且其磁性表现为各向异性磁交叉而非体相相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学压力对金属间化合物的晶体结构和物理性质起关键作用，但f族元素格位上的化学替代如何具体影响Ir-Al框架的响应方式尚不清楚。作者希望揭示用更大的4f离子（Sm）替代5f离子（U）时，结构畸变和磁性响应的具体机制。

Method: 作者通过合成Sm₂Ir₈Al₃₁单晶，利用单晶X射线衍射解析其四方√2×√2×1超结构（空间群P4̄2₁m，源自P4₂/nmc平均结构），并与UIr₄Al₁₅对比分析化学压力的结构响应。同时进行磁化率、比热、电阻率和晶格参数测量来表征其物理性质。

Result: Sm₂Ir₈Al₃₁形成四方√2×√2×1超结构（P4̄2₁m）。U→Sm替代产生的化学压力引起强烈各向异性的结构响应：一种Ir配位环境仅发生适度变化，另一种则发生显著局部重排。Al亚晶格主要承载这些畸变，部分Al位点呈现位置劈裂、各向异性原子位移椭球和部分占有，导致富Al组分约Sm₂Ir₈Al₃₁。磁化率在约50 K处出现与磁场取向相关的宽异常，但比热、电阻率和晶格参数均无对应异常，表明该特征更符合各向异性磁交叉而非体相热力学相变。

Conclusion: 化学压力在f元素格位的替代可诱导Al亚晶格重构并改变磁响应，且在紧密相关的Ir-Al框架内表现为各向异性而非均匀膨胀，揭示了大离子替代驱动结构畸变与磁性演化的耦合机制。

Abstract: Chemical pressure plays a critical role in determining the crystal structures and physical properties of intermetallic compounds. Here, we report the synthesis, crystal structure, and physical properties of Sm$_2$Ir$_8$Al$_{31}$, a structural analog of UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$, in which the smaller 5f uranium atoms are replaced by larger 4f samarium atoms. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction reveals a tetragonal $\sqrt{2}\times\sqrt{2}\times1$ superstructure with space group $P\bar{4}2_1m$, derived from a UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$-like average structure with space group $P4_2/nmc$. Comparison with UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$ shows that chemical pressure from U-to-Sm substitution produces a strongly anisotropic structural response rather than a uniform expansion of the Ir-Al framework. While one Ir coordination environment undergoes only modest changes, the other exhibits substantial local rearrangement. The Al sublattice primarily accommodates these structural distortions, with selected Al sites showing positional splitting, anisotropic atomic displacement ellipsoids, and partial occupancies, resulting in an Al-rich composition of approximately Sm$_2$Ir$_8$Al$_{31}$. Magnetic susceptibility measurements reveal a direction dependence, including a broad anomaly near 50 K for selected magnetic-field orientations. However, the absence of corresponding anomalies in heat capacity, electrical resistivity, and lattice parameters suggests that this feature is more consistent with an anisotropic magnetic crossover than a bulk thermodynamic phase transition. These results highlight how chemical substitution at the f-element site can induce Al-sublattice reconstruction and modify the magnetic response within a closely related Ir-Al structural framework.

</details>
