### [121] [Bayesian Inference in Partially Observed Critical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35973)
*Mihika Singhania*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究：要从大型随机系统中推断全局平均并控制不确定性，需要观测多少样本。作者指出存在一个由单次平衡态抽取导致的"下限误差"（floor），即使全面普查也无法突破；并以随机置换洗牌、二维 Ising 模型和传感网络相关场为例进行了推导与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计推断认为通过增加观测样本量 m 即可使不确定性随 1/√m 无限缩小，但作者发现这忽略了另一个无法通过采样消除的误差来源：观测到的整体配置本身只是平衡分布的一次实现。因此需要定量刻画这一"统计下限"（floor），厘清采样误差与内在波动误差的界限。

Method: 以随机置换洗牌为例，利用共轭贝叶斯估计器，从 m 张均匀抽样卡牌估计平均位移，得到相对不确定性 1/√(2m)（与牌组规模 n 无关）；再通过全方差定律（law of total variance）分离出第二个误差项——下限 R_n，并解析推导出 R_n=(2n²+7)/(5(n-1)²(n+1))。进而将该框架应用于二维 Ising 模型临界点、平均场极限以及相关场传感网络，比较置信区间对总体均值的实际覆盖率，并讨论单次实现下估计下限所需的相关衰减假设。

Result: 随机置换洗牌中，采样相对误差为 1/√(2m)，且后验保持校准；其下限 R_n 具有闭式表达式。二维 Ising 模型临界点处，能量的下限趋于零，而磁化强度的下限保持在约 0.072，其平均场极限为 √2−1。对于传感网络读取的相关场，常规 95% 置信区间实际只覆盖总体均值的 7% 的情形。仅从单次实现估计下限（如单链蒙特卡洛诊断）需依赖相关性衰减假设。

Conclusion: 在从大型随机系统推断全局平均时，除了随采样规模减小的统计误差外，还存在一个由单次平衡态观测决定的下限误差，无法通过增加样本或全面普查消除。正确校准不确定性必须显式考虑这一下限，而仅凭单次实现对其估计则需额外假设相关性的衰减方式。

Abstract: We ask how much of a large random system must be observed before a global average can be inferred with controlled uncertainty. For the random transposition shuffle, a conjugate Bayesian estimator of the average displacement, computed from $m$ uniformly sampled cards, has relative uncertainty $1/\sqrt{2m}$, independent of the deck size $n$. The law of total variance exposes a second error, the floor $R_n$, which arises because the observed configuration is a single draw from equilibrium: no relative tolerance below $κ\sqrt{R_n}$ can be certified, even by a census. For the shuffle we derive $R_n=(2n^2+7)/\bigl(5(n-1)^2(n+1)\bigr)$ and show that the posterior remains calibrated. At the critical point of the two-dimensional Ising model the floor of the energy vanishes, whereas that of the magnetisation stays near $0.072$; its mean-field limit is $\sqrt{2}-1$. For correlated fields read by sensor networks, the usual 95\% interval covers the ensemble mean in 7\% of cases. Bounding the floor from one realisation, as in single-chain Monte Carlo diagnostics, requires an assumption on the decay of correlations.

</details>


### [122] [Cutting, Shuffling and Diffusion as a Coarsening Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35979)
*Daniel Lester,Tanguy LeBorgne,Joris Heyman*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明切割-混洗-扩散（CSD）是一种粗化过程，其混合尺度为 L(n_c Pe/2)^{-1/3} 而非 Batchelor 尺度；切割在波数空间中等价于分数阶拉普拉斯算子，并给出精确混合速率 γ=2^{1/3}|a_1'|n_c^{2/3}λ Pe^{-1/3}，其中 a_1'=-1.0188… 是 Airy 函数导数 Ai' 的第一个零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切割与混洗（CS）能在不拉伸的情况下重排流体，但实现均匀化仍需扩散（D），而 CS 与 D 的相互作用机制此前未被充分理解；作者旨在揭示这一耦合过程的标度律、精确混合速率及其背后的普适类别。

Method: 将 CSD 建模为粗化过程，证明其尺度随 Pe 呈 -1/3 幂次（而非 Batchelor 的 Pe^{-1/2}）；证明切割算子在高频波数域表现为分数阶拉普拉斯算子；利用 Airy 函数导数 Ai' 的第一个零点 a_1'=-1.0188… 推导精确混合速率公式 γ=2^{1/3}|a_1'|n_c^{2/3}λ Pe^{-1/3}，其中 n_c 为每次事件的切割次数、λ 为切割速率，结论对任意维度的随机与确定性协议以及有限时间混合均成立。

Result: 得到 CSD 的混合尺度 \bar ℓ = L(n_c Pe/2)^{-1/3}，精确混合速率 γ=2^{1/3}|a_1'|n_c^{2/3}λ Pe^{-1/3}（a_1'=-1.0188…）作为唯一普适参数；验证该尺度与速率在任意维度、随机/确定性切割协议及有限时间均成立，说明切割以分数阶拉普拉斯形式作用于扩散。

Conclusion: 切割-混洗-扩散过程被确立为一种粗化过程，其特征尺度为 Pe^{-1/3} 而非 Batchelor 尺度；其精确混合速率仅依赖 Airy 函数导数首个零点这一普适常数，使 CSD 归入已知的非平衡普适类，为理解无拉伸流体混合与扩散的耦合提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Cutting and shuffling (CS) rearranges fluid without stretching, but homogenisation requires diffusion (D); this interaction is not well understood. We show that CSD is a coarsening process, with scale $\bar\ell=L(n_c\mathrm{Pe}/2)^{-1/3}$ rather than the Batchelor scale, and that cutting acts as a fractional Laplacian in wavenumber, giving the exact mixing rate $γ=2^{1/3}|a_1'|n_c^{2/3}λ\mathrm{Pe}^{-1/3}$ for $n_c$ cuts per event at rate $λ$, with $a_1'=-1.0188\dots$ the first zero of $\mathrm{Ai}'$. This holds in any dimension for random and deterministic protocols, extends to finite-time mixing, and places CSD in a known non-equilibrium universality class.

</details>


### [123] [Phase Transitions in Mismatched Bayesian Inference for the Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36041)
*Xinyu Sun,Zhou-Quan Wan,Shao-Kai Jian,Hong Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究贝叶斯推断中模型不匹配导致的“过度自信”相：后验分布高度一致却未能忠实重建真实状态。作者以顺磁相伊辛模型的有噪键测量为研究对象，用复本场论解析证明当推断低估测量噪声或高估先验温度（即 β_Q > β）时会出现过度自信相，并通过变量变换将其映射到关联随机键伊辛模型，给出相图对应关系，最后通过三维蒙特卡洛模拟验证了有限大小的过度自信区域，并提出可直接从测量记录反推真实参数以校准推断模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当推断所用模型与真实生成模型不匹配时，贝叶斯推断可能出现“后验一致却不正确”的过度自信现象；理解这种情况何时发生、如何表征，对于实际统计推断的可靠性与模型校准至关重要。作者希望从统计物理角度定量刻画过度自信相出现的条件及其相图结构。

Method: 以顺磁相伊辛模型为真实系统，通过有噪键测量获取数据；推断时假设参数为 β_Q 和 Δ_Q。运用复本场论（replica field theory）进行解析分析，并通过精确变量变换将问题映射到关联随机键伊辛模型（correlated random-bond Ising model），从而建立信息相、无信息相、过度自信相与铁磁相、顺磁相、自旋玻璃相之间的对应关系。进一步对映射模型做三维蒙特卡洛模拟加以验证。

Result: 解析表明，当推断低估测量噪声（Δ_Q < Δ）或假设先验温度低于真实值（β_Q > β，即模型“偏冷”）时会出现过度自信相。变量变换后，信息相对应铁磁相、无信息相对应顺磁相、过度自信相对应自旋玻璃相。三维蒙特卡洛模拟确认在信息相与无信息相之间存在一个有限的过度自信区域，与分析预测一致。

Conclusion: 模型不匹配（尤其低估噪声或先验温度设定不当）会导致贝叶斯推断进入过度自信相；该相可理解为自旋玻璃相。作者还说明真实模型参数可直接从测量记录中推断出来，为实际中校准推断模型提供了一条自然途径。

Abstract: Bayesian inference with a mismatched model can develop an overconfident phase, characterized by posterior agreement without faithful reconstruction of the true state. We study Bayesian inference from noisy bond measurements of an Ising model in its paramagnetic phase, with true inverse temperature $β$ and measurement-noise strength $Δ$, while inference assumes corresponding parameters $β_Q$ and $Δ_Q$. Using replica field theory, we analytically show that the overconfidence phase can occur when inference underestimates the measurement noise ($Δ_Q < Δ$) or assumes a lower prior temperature than the true one ($β_Q > β$). An exact change of variables maps the problem to a correlated random-bond Ising model, where the informative, uninformative, and overconfident phases correspond to ferromagnetic, paramagnetic, and spin-glass phases, respectively. We further perform Monte Carlo simulations of the mapped model in three dimensions and establish a finite overconfident region between the informative and uninformative phases, consistent with our analytical prediction. Finally, we show that the true model parameters can be inferred directly from the measurement records, providing a natural route to calibrating the inference model.

</details>


### [124] [Typicality of entanglement growth and transport fluctuations in quantum symmetric exclusion processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36048)
*Ewan McCulloch*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用宏观涨落理论（MFT）证明量子对称排斥过程（Q-SSEP）中电荷转移统计与纠缠动力学在单条噪声实现下的典型性：长时间电荷转移的全计数统计几乎必然等于经典对称排斥过程（SSEP）的结果，并据此确立了协方差矩阵谱分布及Rényi/von Neumann纠缠熵的自平均性，给出了畴壁与Néel初态下纠缠增长的渐近精确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对Q-SSEP等扩散型自由费米子模型的研究多聚焦于稳态区域，而对瞬态涨落、特别是有限时间内单条实现（而非系综平均）的典型行为缺乏严格刻画。作者希望将宏观涨落理论推广到热力学极限下的瞬态涨落，证明电荷转移与纠缠等可观测量在几乎必然意义下与经典SSEP一致，从而为量子相干长程关联下纠缠动力学的自平均性提供理论基础。

Method: 采用宏观涨落理论，比较电荷转移生成函数与其逆的噪声平均：生成函数的平均退化为经典SSEP；其逆则映射到一个辅助的布朗能量过程，该过程的MFT鞍点问题与SSEP相关联，从而得到匹配的大偏差指数。结合自由费米子恒等式将纠缠与子系统粒子数涨落联系起来，最终确立协方差矩阵本征值分布及纠缠熵的自平均性。研究覆盖平衡态及任意占据对角直积态的淬火初态，并针对畴壁和Néel初态给出具体预言。

Result: 证明在热力学极限的瞬态涨落中，单条噪声实现的长时间电荷转移全计数统计几乎必然与经典SSEP一致，平衡态与淬火后均成立。电荷转移生成函数的噪声平均归约为SSEP，其逆映射到与SSEP鞍点问题等价的辅助布朗能量过程，得到相同的大偏差指数。由此确立协方差矩阵本征值分布和Rényi/von Neumann熵的自平均性，且从畴壁与Néel初态得到的纠缠增长渐近预言与数值模拟高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作将宏观涨落理论成功扩展到瞬态区域，证明了Q-SSEP中电荷转移统计和纠缠动力学的典型性与自平均性：在几乎必然意义下，其长时间行为与经典SSEP一致，且通过自由费米子恒等式将纠缠与粒子数涨落关联，获得了与数值模拟一致的纠缠增长渐近精确预测。

Abstract: We use macroscopic fluctuation theory (MFT) to establish the typicality of charge-transfer statistics and entanglement dynamics in the quantum symmetric exclusion process, a model of stochastically hopping free fermions with diffusive transport and long-range quantum coherence. We show that the leading long-time charge-transfer full counting statistics of individual noise realizations coincide almost surely with those of the classical symmetric exclusion process (SSEP), in equilibrium and following quenches from arbitrary occupation-diagonal product states. Our results apply to transient fluctuations in the thermodynamic limit, extending previous work that has primarily focused on stationary regimes. Our approach compares the noise averages of the charge-transfer generating function and its inverse. The former reduces to SSEP, while the latter maps to an auxiliary Brownian energy process whose MFT saddle-point problem is related to that of SSEP, yielding matching large-deviation exponents. Through free-fermion identities relating entanglement to subsystem-number fluctuations, this establishes the self-averaging of the covariance-matrix eigenvalue distribution and of Rényi and von Neumann entropies. We obtain asymptotically exact predictions for entanglement growth from domain-wall and Néel initial states, in excellent agreement with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [125] [Universal Scaling of Conditional Mutual Information in Classical Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36058)
*Songlei Wang,Chenguang Liang,Zhi-Cheng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维经典随机模型中条件互信息（CMI）的动力学行为，提出马克诺夫长度随时间发散时的普适标度形式，并通过Hankel截断的矩阵乘积态方法在数值上验证，进一步推广到时间域CMI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马克诺夫长度作为CMI的特征衰减长度，最近被识别为混合态相变处的发散长度尺度。然而，马克诺夫长度随时间发散的动力学系统中CMI的标度行为尚不清楚，需要建立统一的标度理论并加以验证。

Method: 作者在一维经典随机模型中研究CMI动力学，提出马克诺夫长度随时间发散的普适标度形式；在宇称守恒有向渗流（PC-DP）的非激活相中，通过畴壁粗化产生的增长长度尺度和自相似概率分布推导该形式；引入基于Hankel的截断方案实现长时间、大空间区域的矩阵乘积态（MPS）模拟；并将框架推广到时间域CMI。

Result: 在非激活相和非平衡临界点均验证了所提出的标度形式，其中临界点处马克诺夫长度增长由动力学临界指数支配；更广泛地，集体推断机制导致的发散马克诺夫长度也由同一标度形式刻画；尽管机制不同，固定微观区域间的CMI表现出共同的标度维数2；时间域CMI满足条件依赖强度与特征记忆时间之间的类似关系。

Conclusion: 作者建立了马克诺夫长度随时间发散的CMI普适标度形式，涵盖畴壁粗化、临界增长和集体推断等不同机制，并通过高效的Hankel截断MPS数值方法加以验证；该框架进一步推广至时间域，揭示了条件依赖强度与特征记忆时间之间的普遍关系。

Abstract: Conditional mutual information (CMI) characterizes dependence between two regions conditioned on an intervening buffer. Its characteristic decay length, the Markov length, has recently been identified as a diverging length scale at mixed-state phase transitions. Here, we investigate CMI dynamics in one-dimensional classical stochastic models and propose a universal scaling form for systems whose Markov length diverges with time. We derive this form in the inactive phase of parity-conserving directed percolation, where domain-wall coarsening generates a growing length scale and a self-similar probability distribution. We introduce a Hankel-based truncation scheme that enables matrix-product-state simulations at long times and over large spatial regions. Using this method, we verify the proposed scaling both in the inactive phase and at the nonequilibrium critical point, where the dynamical critical exponent governs Markov-length growth. More broadly, the same scaling form captures cases in which collective inference sets the diverging Markov length. Despite these distinct mechanisms, the CMI between fixed microscopic regions exhibits a common scaling dimension of two. Finally, we extend this framework to temporal CMI, establishing an analogous relation between the strength of conditional dependence in time domain and its characteristic memory time.

</details>


### [126] [Ratchet Effects for Swarmalators on Asymmetric Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36318)
*B. Albert,A. Libal,C. J. O. Reichhardt,C. Reichhardt*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了Adorjáni提出的主动物质盘状swarmalator模型耦合非对称衬底后的棘轮效应，发现swarmalator相互作用可根据系统结构状态显著增强或抑制主动物质棘轮运动，且棘轮效率取决于衬底强度、衬底晶格间距和马达力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前该模型在无swarmalator规则时高活性下会形成运动诱导相分离(MIPS)态，加入规则后又出现活性凝胶、迷宫态、受阻MIPS和受阻流体等新状态；但尚不清楚这些不同结构状态耦合非对称衬底后如何影响主动物质棘轮输运，因此需要系统考察swarmalator相互作用对棘轮效应的增强或抑制作用。

Method: 作者将Adorjáni的主动物质盘状swarmalator模型与不对称衬底耦合，通过调节衬底强度、衬底晶格间距和粒子上的马达力三个参数，系统测量棘轮效率，并比较凝胶态、迷宫态等不同结构状态下的棘轮输运行为。

Result: swarmalator相互作用能强烈增强或抑制主动物质棘轮输运，具体取决于系统的结构状态；棘轮效率依赖于衬底强度、衬底晶格间距和马达力。凝胶态在小衬底间距时棘轮最弱，甚至可产生反向棘轮运动，但在大马达力下棘轮最强。迷宫态在较宽参数范围内表现出最强棘轮，因为其准一维迷宫形貌能很好地匹配衬底几何结构。

Conclusion: swarmalator内在相位自由度通过改变粒子空间组织形态，显著调控了主动物质在非对称衬底上的定向输运；不同结构状态（凝胶态与迷宫态）在不同参数区间呈现截然相反甚至反转的棘轮行为，说明结构形貌与衬底几何的匹配是决定棘轮效率的关键因素。

Abstract: Swarmalators are particles which have internal degrees of freedom in the form of phase oscillators that can synchronize and change the dynamics of the particles. We explore the active matter disk swarmalator model proposed by Adorjáni [Phys. Rev. E 109, 024607 (2024)] where there is an additional attraction or repulsion determined by whether the phases of particles within a certain radius synchronize or anti-synchronize. This model in the absence of swarmalator rules forms a motility-induced phase-separated (MIPS) state at high activity, but when the swarmalator rules are present, a number of additional states appear including an active gel, labyrinth state, frustrated MIPS, and frustrated fluid states. Here we examine ratchet effects for the Adorjáni model coupled to asymmetric substrates. We find that swarmalator interactions can strongly enhance or suppress active matter ratcheting depending on the structural state of the system, and show that the ratchet efficiency depends on the substrate strength, substrate lattice spacing, and the motor force on the particles. The gel state exhibits the weakest ratcheting for small substrate spacing and can even produce a reversed ratchet motion, but shows the strongest ratcheting for large motor forces. The labyrinth state exhibits the strongest ratcheting over a wide range of parameters since the quasi-one-dimensional labyrinth morphology can match the substrate geometry well.

</details>


### [127] [Constructive Cavity Method for Ising Restricted and Deep Boltzmann Machines with Gaussian Couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36505)
*Simone Franchini,Francesco Concetti,Giampiero Bardella,Liming Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将构造腔方法（CCM）应用于具有高斯层间耦合的稠密 Ising 型受限/深度玻尔兹曼机，借助 Aizenman–Sims–Starr 随机重叠结构识别 cavity 变量的 blanket 分解，并用随机能量模型（REM）普适性假设将腔自由能表示为广义随机能量模型（GREM）的自由能，通过泊松点过程（PPP）递归求解，最终给出有限层构造腔泛函的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是给稠密、Ising 型、多层玻尔兹曼机（RBM/DBM）求得可计算的构造腔自由能表达式，从而由 cavity 方法刻画这类非凸多层模型的热力学，并揭示多层几何结构如何进入自由能函数。

Method: 采用构造腔方法（CCM）：先分离核心（core）与界面（interface）贡献，识别出腔变量的 blanket 分解与 Aizenman–Sims–Starr 随机重叠结构的一致性；再应用 REM 普适性假设，将腔自由能视作广义随机能量模型（GREM）的自由能；随后用泊松点过程（PPP）进行递归求值。逐层规范（layer-wise gauge）下，第 ℓ 个 blanket 的累计并集以自身累计比例 |U_ℓ∩L_p|=λ_p q_ℓ^{(p)}N+o(N) 切割各层，几何信息由矩阵 M=2M_0Λ 与 M_1=ΛM_0Λ 承载，并据此给出腔场与修正场的 blanket 方差。

Result: 得到有限层构造腔泛函 P_{RBM/DBM}^{CCM}(ζ,q)=log2+Σ_{p=1}^K λ_p X_0^{(p)} − ½ Σ_{ℓ=0}^{r−1} ζ_ℓ(q_{ℓ+1}^⊤ M_1 q_{ℓ+1} − q_ℓ^⊤ M_1 q_ℓ)。其中 q_ℓ=(q_ℓ^{(1)},…,q_ℓ^{(K)}) 为各 blanket 的切割比例向量，满足 0=q_0≤q_1≤…≤q_r=1；构造腔场在第 p 层的 blanket 方差增量 Q_ℓ^{(p)}−Q_{ℓ−1}^{(p)}=(M(q_ℓ−q_{ℓ−1}))_p，修正场的方差增量为 q_ℓ^⊤ M_1 q_ℓ − q_{ℓ−1}^⊤ M_1 q_{ℓ−1}。通过施加 q_ℓ=q_ℓ 1 可恢复平衡标量规范。

Conclusion: CCM 结合 REM–GREM 普适性与 PPP 递归，能显式给出稠密多层 Ising 型 RBM/DBM 的有限层构造腔泛函，多层几何的影响完全编码在矩阵 M 与 M_1 中；但平衡标量规范只是特例，在非凸多层区域选取物理值（即确定正确的 q 配置）仍是一个尚未解决的独立问题。

Abstract: We apply the Constructive Cavity Method (CCM) to the dense, Ising type, restricted and deep Boltzmann machine with Gaussian couplings between adjacent layers. After identifying the core and interface contributions, CCM identifies the blanket decomposition of the cavity variables with the Aizenman--Sims--Starr random overlap structure. We apply the Random Energy Model (REM) universality ansatz to represent the cavity free energies by those of a generalized random energy model (GREM) and evaluate them recursively through Poisson point processes (PPPs). In the layer-wise gauge each layer is cut with its own cumulative fraction $|U_\ell\cap L_p|=λ_p q_\ell^{(p)}N+o(N)$, with $\mathbf 0=\mathbf q_0\le\mathbf q_1\le\cdots\le\mathbf q_r=\mathbf 1$, where $\mathbf q_\ell=(q_\ell^{(1)},\ldots,q_\ell^{(K)})$. Here $U_\ell$ is the cumulative blanket union up to blanket $\ell$. The index $p$ labels architectural layers, whereas $\ell$ labels the blankets. The dependence on the multilayer geometry is carried by the two matrices $M=2M_0Λ$ and $M_1=ΛM_0Λ$. The constructive cavity field in layer $p$ has blanket variances $Q_\ell^{(p)}-Q_{\ell-1}^{(p)}=(M(\mathbf q_\ell-\mathbf q_{\ell-1}))_p$, while the correction field has blanket variances $Q_\ell-Q_{\ell-1}=\mathbf q_\ell^\top M_1\mathbf q_\ell-\mathbf q_{\ell-1}^\top M_1\mathbf q_{\ell-1}$. The nested REM--PPP average gives the finite-level constructive cavity functional $\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{RBM/DBM}}^{\mathrm{CCM}}(ζ,\mathbf q)=\log2+\sum_{p=1}^Kλ_pX_0^{(p)}-\frac12\sum_{\ell=0}^{r-1}ζ_\ell(\mathbf q_{\ell+1}^\top M_1\mathbf q_{\ell+1}-\mathbf q_\ell^\top M_1\mathbf q_\ell)$. The balanced scalar gauge is recovered by imposing $\mathbf q_\ell=q_\ell\mathbf 1$. Selecting the physical value in the non-convex multilayer regime is a separate problem.

</details>


### [128] [Kinetic Sectors and Nonequilibrium Dynamics in the $δ$-Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36829)
*Dehen Cao,Wei Jiang,Ya Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了δ-Ising模型——一种动力学伊辛铁磁体，其中单自旋翻转只有在能量变化不超过可调阈值时才被接受。阈值与磁场的联合作用划分出不同的动力学扇区，每个扇区由固定马尔可夫转移算符支配。作者证明当上下自旋拥有等量开放翻转通道时，系统保持全局自旋反演对称性；在周期链上，无偏随机初态因此各以1/2概率到达顺场态与反场态。关闭单条通道后，反向有序的概率按(2/π)^L指数衰减。参数扫描对应扇区算符的有序乘积，使“到达”与“保持”态在不同扇区边界分离，从而产生迟滞。增大系统尺寸可诱发类似Mpemba的反转效应——更多错位自旋的态可能更早到达有序态，其原因在于平均吸收时间对多短畴按对数增长、对单一宏观畴按线性增长，且打开体翻转通道后该效应消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索一个简单直观的动力学规则——以可调能量阈值约束单自旋翻转——如何统一起伏、磁场响应、记忆与弛豫行为，从而揭示局域动力学约束如何决定伊辛模型的宏观非平衡特性。

Method: 定义δ-Ising模型：单自旋翻转若其能量变化不超过阈值δ即被接受。通过变化磁场与阈值打开或关闭局域翻转通道，将参数空间划分为若干动力学扇区，每个扇区由固定的马尔可夫转移算符刻画。利用扇区算符的有序乘积表征参数扫描过程；在周期链上通过解析分析与数值计算研究吸收概率、平均吸收时间随系统尺寸的标度。

Result: 当上下自旋的开通通道数相等时，全局自旋反演对称性严格保持，无偏初态以各1/2概率到达顺场与反场态；关闭单条通道后两者仍为吸收态，但反向有序概率按(2/π)^L衰减。迟滞现象源于“到达”与“释放”发生在不同的扇区边界。系统尺寸增大可导致Mpemba类反转：平均吸收时间对多短畴构型呈对数增长、对单一宏观畴呈线性增长；该反转在打开体翻转通道后消失。

Conclusion: δ-Ising模型表明，一个简单的局域动力学规则即可将动力学约束、磁场响应、记忆效应与反常弛豫统一于同一框架，为理解受约束伊辛动力学的非平衡行为提供了分析范例。

Abstract: We introduce and analyze the $δ$-Ising model, a kinetic Ising ferromagnet in which single-spin flips are accepted when their energy change does not exceed a tunable threshold. Varying the field and threshold opens or closes local flip channels, dividing parameter space into kinetic sectors, each governed by a fixed Markov transition operator. We prove that the dynamics retains global spin-inversion symmetry exactly when up and down spins have equal numbers of open channels. In one such sector on a periodic chain of length $L$, unbiased random initial states therefore reach the field-aligned and antifield states with probability $1/2$ each. Closing a single channel leaves both states absorbing but makes the probability of ordering against the field decay as $(2/π)^L$. Parameter sweeps correspond to ordered products of sector operators, which separate reaching a state from holding it, so hysteresis appears when a state is reached and released at different sector boundaries. We also show how increasing the system size can induce a Mpemba-like reversal, allowing a state with more misaligned spins to reach the final ordered state sooner on average. On a periodic chain, this reversal arises because the mean absorption time grows logarithmically with system size for many short domains but linearly for a single macroscopic domain, and it disappears when the bulk flip channel is opened. These findings show how a simple, intuitive rule for Ising dynamics links local kinetic constraints to field response, memory, and relaxation within a unified framework.

</details>


### [129] [Stability reversal, activated escape, and mechanical response of negative-mobility particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36884)
*Junteng Li,Zhihong You*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究负迁移率如何重塑粒子在机械势阱中的随机行为：负迁移率使势阱最小值失稳，产生双峰局域化，并将经典Kramers逃逸变为两阶段过程；在外加均匀力作用下，有效双阱发生平移而非倾斜，弱噪声下响应由阱内弛豫主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解粒子在势能景观中的涨落与响应是非平衡统计物理的基本问题。传统研究多假设正迁移率，而对负迁移率（弱力下为负、强力下为正）如何改变机械势中的随机稳定性、逃逸过程与对外力的响应尚不清楚，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 考虑一维过阻尼粒子受加性噪声作用，其迁移率在弱力时为负、强力时为正。将该粒子置于机械势阱中，分析负迁移率对势能最小值的失稳作用，以及在外加空间均匀力下有效双阱的平移行为，并研究弱噪声下的弛豫与响应机制。

Result: 负迁移率使机械势最小值失稳，并在两个偏离中心的位置产生双峰局域化；经典Kramers逃逸被转变为包含中间亚稳态的两阶段逃逸过程；外加空间均匀力时，机械扰动使有效双阱发生平移而非倾斜，弱噪声下响应由阱内弛豫主导，而自发位置涨落则由慢的阱间切换主导。

Conclusion: 负迁移率深刻重塑了机械势景观、随机稳定性与响应之间的相互作用，揭示出与正迁移率情形截然不同的行为，如双峰局域化、两阶段逃逸以及由阱内弛豫主导的正响应。

Abstract: Understanding the fluctuations and response of particles in potential landscapes is a fundamental problem in nonequilibrium statistical physics. Here, we investigate how negative mobility reshapes the stochastic behavior of a particle moving in a mechanical potential landscape. We consider a one-dimensional overdamped particle subject to additive noise, with mobility that is negative at weak forces and positive at strong forces. In a potential well, negative mobility destabilizes the mechanical potential minimum and produces bimodal localization at two off-center positions. The same stability reversal transforms conventional Kramers escape from a potential well into a two-stage escape process involving an intermediate metastable state. When an additional spatially uniform force is applied, the mechanical perturbation translates rather than tilts the effective double well, yielding a positive response dominated at weak noise by intrawell relaxation, even though slow interwell switching dominates spontaneous positional fluctuations. These findings reveal how negative mobility reshapes the interplay among mechanical landscapes, stochastic stability, and response.

</details>


### [130] [Residual dynamics determine dissipation in strongly coupled linear Langevin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37030)
*Vansh Kharbanda,Anton F. Burnet,Benedikt Sabass*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究耦合对非平衡系统熵产生率（EPR）的影响，证明强耦合会抑制直接耦合自由度上的不可逆环流，在二维全同系统中使EPR单调下降并在强耦合极限下趋于零电流；在高维或非全同系统中，EPR趋于由残余自由度动力学决定的强耦合极限，并据此给出耦合变量选择是否降耗散的判据，最终在毛细胞束与细菌趋化模型中验证了该预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多物理与生物非平衡系统的性质由相互作用塑造，但集体运作究竟提高还是降低熵产生率（耗散）一直难以预测，本文旨在厘清耦合强度与不可逆环流、熵产生率之间的定量关系。

Method: 作者通过理论分析耦合对不可逆环流与概率流（probability currents）的影响：在二维全同系统中证明强耦合对非零耦合模式的环流抑制是单调的，进而导致EPR单调下降并于强耦合极限下电流消失；在高维情形下将系统分解为直接耦合自由度与残余自由度，考察残余动力学所维持的不可逆电流如何决定EPR的强耦合极限，并分析有限耦合修正电流与残余电流的相对取向对收敛方式的影响；进一步将结论推广到非全同耦合系统，并用毛细胞束（hair-cell bundles）与细菌趋化（bacterial chemotaxis）的非线性模型作数值验证。

Result: 强耦合抑制直接耦合自由度上的不可逆环流；全同二维系统中该抑制对非零耦合模式单调，使EPR单调下降且相应概率流在强耦合极限下消失；高维系统中残余自由度的不可逆电流仍可持续，EPR趋于由残余动力学设定的强耦合极限，从而提供判断某种耦合变量选择是否比未耦合系统耗散更低的判据；有限耦合电流修正与残余电流的对齐方式决定趋近该极限的方式；收敛到残余动力学的结论同样适用于非全同耦合系统；非线性毛细胞束与细菌趋化模型验证了EPR降低的预测。

Conclusion: 强耦合通过抑制直接耦合自由度的不可逆环流从而降低熵产生率，残余自由度的耗散决定强耦合极限，据此可判断耦合变量选择是否减耗；该机制在生物传感系统（毛细胞束、细菌趋化）等非线性模型中成立。

Abstract: Interactions shape the properties of many physical and biological nonequilibrium systems, but whether collective operation raises or lowers entropy production remains difficult to predict. Here, we show that strong coupling suppresses irreversible circulation associated with the directly coupled degrees of freedom. In identical two-dimensional systems, this suppression is monotonic for nonzero coupling modes and guarantees a monotonic decrease of the entropy production rate (EPR), with the corresponding probability currents vanishing in the strong-coupling limit. In higher dimensions, suppressing the circulation associated with the coupled degrees of freedom does not generally eliminate all irreversible circulation, since the remaining degrees of freedom can still sustain nonequilibrium currents. The EPR of these residual dynamics sets the strong-coupling limit, providing a criterion for whether a given choice of coupled variables leads to lower dissipation than the uncoupled system. The approach to this limit depends on how the finite-coupling current correction aligns with the residual current. Further, we show that convergence to residual dynamics in the strong-coupling limit also applies to coupled non-identical systems. Finally, we demonstrate the predicted EPR reduction in nonlinear models of vertebrate hair-cell bundles and bacterial chemotaxis.

</details>


### [131] [Langevin dynamics towards Generalized Gibbs Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37129)
*Rafael González-Albaladejo,Leticia F. Cugliandolo,Gustavo S. Lozano,Daniel A. Stariolo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 作者提出将广义吉布斯系综(GGE)重新解释为另一种非标准力学模型的玻尔兹曼测度，从而可用随机方法（如朗之万过程）进行采样。他们以可积的诺伊曼模型为基准，验证了经适当调谐的朗之万过程能够复现淬火后孤立系统渐近动力学的可观测量值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来统计物理工具被用于研究宏观量子与经典可积模型的动力学，结果表明热力学极限下局域可观测量的长时间平均等于用广义吉布斯系综(GGE)计算的系综平均。但GGE涉及所有守恒量及其拉格朗日乘子，直接采样困难，因此需要一种能将GGE转化为可实际采样的概率测度的方法。

Method: 将GGE重新解释为一个非标准力学模型的玻尔兹曼测度，从而可以用随机方法（如朗之万过程）进行采样。作者专门设计朗之万方程中的确定性力，使得随机轨迹在长时间后采样到目标测度。以可积的诺伊曼模型（球面上各向异性谐振子势约束下的经典粒子）为基准进行验证；测试设置在淬火后孤立诺伊曼模型渐近动力学所确定的拉格朗日乘子条件下。

Result: 该问题依初始条件和淬火协议的不同呈现不同的相，但在所有情形下，经适当调谐的朗之万过程的随机演化都能复现相关可观测量的渐近值，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 将GGE解释为玻尔兹曼测度并用调谐朗之万过程采样的方法是可行的，能够在不同相区准确重现淬火后可积模型的渐近统计性质，为采样GGE提供了一条实用的随机动力学途径。

Abstract: In recent years, statistical physics tools have been used to study the dynamics of macroscopic quantum and classical integrable models. These studies have shown that, in the thermodynamic limit, long term time-averages of local observables equal statistical averages calculated with a Generalized Gibbs Ensemble (GGE). In the latter, the weight in phase space involves all constants of motion multiplied by Lagrange multipliers which are fixed by their initial values. We argue that the GGE can be interpreted as a proper Boltzmann measure of a different (non-standard) mechanical model. This measure can therefore be sampled with stochastic methods implemented, for example, as a Langevin process. We tailor the deterministic force in the Langevin equations so that at long times the stochastic trajectories sample the desired measure. We use the integrable Neumann Model -- the model of a classical particle constrained to move on a sphere under anisotropic harmonic potentials -- as a benchmark. We test the method in a setup where the Lagrange multipliers are fixed by the GGE governing the asymptotic dynamics of the isolated Neumann model following a quench. This problem has different phases depending on the initial conditions and quench protocol and, in all cases, with the stochastic evolution of an adequately tuned Langevin process we reproduce the asymptotic values of relevant observables.

</details>


### [132] [Magnetic structures in a model nickelate: Monte Carlo study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37294)
*V. S. Ryumshin,Yu. D. Panov,A. S. Moskvin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在经典蒙特卡洛方法框架下，结合恒定玻色子浓度的运动学约束，研究了一种模型镍酸盐的磁性结构，并确定了不同交换积分（考虑至第三近邻）下的磁结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索模型镍酸盐在不同交换积分条件下可能形成的磁性结构，以理解其磁有序行为与交换相互作用之间的关系。

Method: 采用经典蒙特卡洛方法，并考虑恒定玻色子浓度的运动学约束；在模型中纳入至第三近邻的交换积分，通过调节这些交换参数确定对应的磁基态结构。

Result: 确定了模型镍酸盐在不同交换积分取值（包含至第三近邻）下的多种磁性结构，给出了各参数区域与相应磁有序态的对应关系。

Conclusion: 交换积分（特别是远邻交换作用）的取值对模型镍酸盐的磁结构具有决定性影响，考虑至第三近邻的相互作用对于准确描述其磁基态是必要的。

Abstract: Within the framework of the classical Monte Carlo method with kinematic account of constant bosons concentration a model nickelate was studied, its magnetic structures for various values of exchange integrals considering up to third-nearest neighbours were determined.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum critical scaling in convective active matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37569)
*Jamir Marino*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在 $d=2-\epsilon$ 维度下，利用单圈重整化群构建了临界量子活性物质的最小场论：一个带自对流（self-advection）的非守恒标量序参量，服从 Landau-Ginzburg 序化。结果显示上临界维数为 $d_c=2$，并发现两个重整化群不动点：一个是纯耗散不动点（非线性噪声抑制对流），另一个是量子对流鞍点（相互作用、对流与非高斯噪声共存）。作者由此给出临界指数，并论证该对流区区别于通常的 Burgers 流体力学，可调控量子活性物质微观模型中过渡到半经典动力学前的瞬态普适标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为临界量子活性物质建立一个最小（且可重整化群分析）的场论框架，厘清序参数非守恒性、自对流、扩散噪声与高阶量子顶点及非高斯噪声涨落之间的竞争关系，从而在原理上刻画量子活性体系临界行为的普适类。

Method: 构建非守恒标量序参数的 Landau-Ginzburg 场论并引入自对流项；在扩散噪声主导、高阶量子顶点与非高斯噪声涨落保持相关的条件下，于上临界维数 $d_c=2$ 附近做 $d=2-\epsilon$ 维单圈重整化群分析，寻找并分类不动点。

Result: 得到两个不动点：纯耗散不动点（非线性噪声抑制对流）与量子对流鞍点（相互作用、对流、非高斯噪声共存）；确定相应临界指数；证明量子对流区与经典 Burgers 流体力学在普适类上不同，并指出它可在体系渡越到半经典动力学之前主导瞬态普适标度。

Conclusion: 该最小场论揭示量子活性物质临界点存在由非高斯噪声、对流和相互作用共同决定的非平凡普适行为，其上临界维数为 2；对流机制构成独立于 Burgers 流体力学的新普适类，并能描述体系进入半经典动力学前的瞬态标度。

Abstract: We construct a minimal field theory for critical quantum active matter, describing a nonconserved scalar order parameter with Landau-Ginzburg ordering and self-advection. When diffusive noise dominates, higher-order quantum vertices and non-Gaussian noise fluctuations remain relevant alongside the deterministic nonlinearities, with an upper critical dimension $d_c=2$. A one-loop renormalization-group analysis in $d=2-ε$ reveals a purely dissipative fixed point, where nonlinear noise suppresses convection, and a quantum convective saddle, where interactions, convection, and non-Gaussian noise coexist. We determine the critical exponents, show that the convective regime is distinct from conventional Burgers hydrodynamics, and discuss how it could govern transient universal scaling in microscopic models of quantum active matter before a crossover to semi-classical dynamics.

</details>
